THEOREM Trefoil.LatticeArithmetic::Trefoil.smallWeight_long_blocks
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {m b c g ell rB sB tB rC sC tC : ℤ},
  m = 4 ∨ 6 ≤ m →
    m * b ^ 2 + (g - 1) ^ 2 + 2 * g ^ 2 + 2 * rB ^ 2 + 2 * sB ^ 2 + tB ^ 2 = 6 →
      m * c ^ 2 + 2 * ell ^ 2 + (ell + 1) ^ 2 + 2 * rC ^ 2 + 2 * sC ^ 2 + tC ^ 2 = 3 →
        c = 0 ∧ (b = 0 ∨ m = 4 ∧ b ^ 2 = 1)
THEOREM Trefoil.LatticeArithmetic::Trefoil.weightFour_long_block
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {m a z r s t : ℤ},
  m = 4 ∨ 6 ≤ m →
    m * a ^ 2 + 2 * a + 1 + 3 * z ^ 2 + 2 * r ^ 2 + 2 * s ^ 2 + t ^ 2 = 4 →
      (a = 0 ∨ m = 4 ∧ a = -1) ∧ (m * a ^ 2 + 2 * a + 1 = 1 ∨ m * a ^ 2 + 2 * a + 1 = 3)
THEOREM Trefoil.LatticeArithmetic::Trefoil.weightFour_weightThree_shapes
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {k z ell rA sA tA rC sC tC : ℤ},
  k = 1 ∨ k = 3 →
    k + 3 * z ^ 2 + 2 * rA ^ 2 + 2 * sA ^ 2 + tA ^ 2 = 4 →
      2 * ell ^ 2 + (ell + 1) ^ 2 + 2 * rC ^ 2 + 2 * sC ^ 2 + tC ^ 2 = 3 →
        z * (3 * ell + 1) + 2 * (rA * rC + sA * sC) + tA * tC = 0 →
          ell = 0 ∧ z = 0 ∧ tA ^ 2 = 1 ∧ tC = 0 ∧ rC ^ 2 + sC ^ 2 = 1
THEOREM Trefoil.LatticeArithmetic::Trefoil.weightSix_shape
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {d g r s t : ℤ},
  d = 0 ∨ d = 4 →
    d + (g - 1) ^ 2 + 2 * g ^ 2 + 2 * r ^ 2 + 2 * s ^ 2 + t ^ 2 = 6 →
      ¬((3 * g - 1) % 2 = 0 ∧ t % 2 = 0) → g = 0 ∧ t ^ 2 = 1 ∧ (d = 0 → r ^ 2 = 1 ∧ s ^ 2 = 1) ∧ (d = 4 → r = 0 ∧ s = 0)

THEOREM Trefoil.LatticeOddBasis::Trefoil.LatticeOddBasis.no_odd_basis_embedding
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {m : ℕ},
  m = 4 ∨ 6 ≤ m →
    ∀ (M : Matrix (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m) (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m) ℤ),
      Odd M.det →
        ∀ (choose : Option (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m) → Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m),
          Function.Injective choose →
            ∀ (A C : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Vector m),
              (∀ (r s : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
                  Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot (M.col (choose (some r))) (M.col (choose (some s))) =
                    Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.rootGram r s) →
                ∑ j, A j ^ 2 = 4 →
                  ∑ j, M.col (choose none) j ^ 2 = 6 →
                    ∑ j, C j ^ 2 = 3 →
                      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot A (M.col (choose none)) = -1 →
                        Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot A C = 0 →
                          Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot (M.col (choose none)) C = 0 →
                            (∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
                                Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot A (M.col (choose (some r))) =
                                  Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.aRootPairing r) →
                              (∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
                                  Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot (M.col (choose none)) (M.col (choose (some r))) =
                                    Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.bRootPairing r) →
                                (∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
                                    Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot C (M.col (choose (some r))) =
                                      Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.cRootPairing r) →
                                  False

THEOREM Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior::Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.checkSnapshot_of_tables
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ (D : Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.SignedPD) (I : Array (List Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.HalfEdge))
  (neighbor cuspLabels : Array ℕ) (mu para lambda writhe : Array ℤ),
  D.incidence = I →
    D.checkWith I = true →
      (Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.givenFaces D I == neighbor) = true →
        Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.givenCusps D = cuspLabels →
          Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.givenMeridians D I = some mu →
            Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.givenParallel D = para →
              Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.givenWrithe D = writhe →
                (Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.correctLongitudes mu para cuspLabels writhe == lambda) = true →
                  Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.checkSnapshot D neighbor cuspLabels mu lambda = true

TYPE ∀ {ι : Type u_1} {I : Type u_2} [inst : AddCommGroup I] [inst_1 : _root_.Module ℤ I] (coordinates : I ≃ₗ[ℤ] ι → ℤ)
  (x : I) (i : ι), (Trefoil.Section3ACoefficients.coordinateReduction coordinates) x i = ↑(coordinates x i)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section3ACoefficients::Trefoil.Section3ACoefficients.coordinateReduction_eq_zero
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {ι : Type u_1} {I : Type u_2} [inst : AddCommGroup I] [inst_1 : _root_.Module ℤ I] (coordinates : I ≃ₗ[ℤ] ι → ℤ)
  (x : I), (Trefoil.Section3ACoefficients.coordinateReduction coordinates) x = 0 ↔ ∃ y, x = y + y

AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

THEOREM Trefoil.Section3ACoefficients::Trefoil.Section3ACoefficients.reduced_columns_independent
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {ι : Type u_1} {β : Type u_4} {κ : Type u_5} [inst : DecidableEq β] {P : Type u_6} {X : Type u_7} {P₂ : Type u_8}
  {X₂ : Type u_9} [inst_1 : AddCommGroup P] [inst_2 : _root_.Module ℤ P] [inst_3 : AddCommGroup X]
  [inst_4 : _root_.Module ℤ X] [inst_5 : AddCommGroup P₂] [inst_6 : _root_.Module (ZMod 2) P₂]
  [inst_7 : AddCommGroup X₂] [inst_8 : _root_.Module (ZMod 2) X₂] (pieceCoordinates : P ≃ₗ[ℤ] β → ℤ)
  (closedCoordinates : X ≃ₗ[ℤ] κ → ℤ) (includeIntegral : P →ₗ[ℤ] X) (includeModTwo : P₂ →ₗ[ZMod 2] X₂)
  (pieceReduce : P →+ P₂),
  Function.Surjective ⇑pieceReduce →
    (∀ (x : P), pieceReduce x = 0 ↔ ∃ y, x = y + y) →
      ∀ (closedReduce : X →+ X₂),
        Function.Surjective ⇑closedReduce →
          (∀ (x : X), closedReduce x = 0 ↔ ∃ y, x = y + y) →
            (∀ (x : P), closedReduce (includeIntegral x) = includeModTwo (pieceReduce x)) →
              Function.Injective ⇑includeModTwo →
                ∀ (selected : ι → β),
                  Function.Injective selected →
                    LinearIndependent (ZMod 2) fun i j =>
                      ↑(closedCoordinates (includeIntegral (pieceCoordinates.symm (Pi.single (selected i) 1))) j)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section3ACoefficients::Trefoil.Section3ACoefficients.reductionLinearEquiv_apply
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {ι : Type u_1} {I : Type u_2} {M : Type u_3} [inst : AddCommGroup I] [inst_1 : _root_.Module ℤ I]
  [inst_2 : AddCommGroup M] [inst_3 : _root_.Module (ZMod 2) M] (coordinates : I ≃ₗ[ℤ] ι → ℤ) (reduce : I →+ M)
  (surj : Function.Surjective ⇑reduce) (kernel : ∀ (x : I), reduce x = 0 ↔ ∃ y, x = y + y) (x : I) (i : ι),
  (Trefoil.Section3ACoefficients.reductionLinearEquiv coordinates reduce surj kernel) (reduce x) i = ↑(coordinates x i)

  1 ≤ m →
    ∃ G,
      T.boundary G.X = T.reverse (T.lens (104 * m + 37) 104) ∧
        Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Positive T G.X ∧
          ∀ (i j : Fin (Trefoil.Section3APlumbing.plumbingRank m)),
            ((T.intersection G.X) (G.handle i)) (G.handle j) = Trefoil.Section3APlumbing.plumbingGram m i j

AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {T : Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Geometry} (R : Trefoil.Section3ATopology.References T) {G W : T.Four}
  (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)),
  T.simplyConnected G →
    T.simplyConnected W →
      Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Positive T G →
        Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Positive T W →
          ∀ {p q : ℕ},
            T.boundary W = T.lens p q →
              0 < q → q < p → p.Coprime q → Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Positive T (R.glue G W h).X

    T.simplyConnected W →
      ∀ {p q : ℕ},

            q < p →

                Function.Injective ⇑(R.glue G W h).inclZ ∧

AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  m = 4 ∨ 6 ≤ m →
    ∀ (G : Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Handlebody T (Trefoil.Section3APlumbing.plumbingRank m))
      (W : Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Handlebody T 4),
      T.boundary G.X = T.reverse (T.lens (104 * m + 37) 104) →

                    ((T.intersection W.X) (W.handle (Trefoil.Section3APlumbing.owensRoot i)))
                        (W.handle (Trefoil.Section3APlumbing.owensRoot j)) =
                      if i = j then 2 else 0) →
                  False

AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

THEOREM Trefoil.LatticeBlocks::_private.Trefoil.LatticeBlocks.0.Trefoil.LatticeBlocks.finite_signed_chain
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (k : ℕ),
  1 ≤ k →
    ∀ (v : Fin k → ι → ℤ),
      (∀ (i : Fin k), ∑ a, v i a ^ 2 = 2) →
        (Function.Injective fun i a => ↑(v i a)) →
          (∀ (i j : Fin k), ↑i + 1 = ↑j → Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot (v i) (v j) = -1) →
            (∀ (i j : Fin k), ↑i + 1 < ↑j → Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot (v i) (v j) = 0) →
              ∃ p eps,
                (∀ (i : Fin k), Trefoil.LatticeSupports.support (v i) = {p ↑i, p (↑i + 1)}) ∧
                  (∀ (i j : ℕ), i ≤ k → j ≤ k → p i = p j → i = j) ∧
                    (∀ j ≤ k, eps j ^ 2 = 1) ∧
                      ∀ (i : Fin k) (x : ι → ℤ),
                        Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot x (v i) = eps ↑i * x (p ↑i) - eps (↑i + 1) * x (p (↑i + 1))
THEOREM Trefoil.LatticeBlocks::_private.Trefoil.LatticeBlocks.0.Trefoil.LatticeBlocks.long_path
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

THEOREM Trefoil.FiniteCorrectionTerms::Trefoil.Finite.correctionTerm.eq_1
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ (p q i : ℕ),
  Trefoil.Finite.correctionTerm p q i =
    if hq : q = 0 then 0
    else (↑p * ↑q - (2 * ↑i + 1 - ↑p - ↑q) ^ 2) / (4 * ↑p * ↑q) - Trefoil.Finite.correctionTerm q (p % q) (i % q)

THEOREM Trefoil.LatticeBlocks::Trefoil.LatticeBlocks.Frame.pairing
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {m : ℕ} {E : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Embedding m} (self : Trefoil.LatticeBlocks.Frame E)
  (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m) (x : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m → ℤ),
  Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot x (E.root r) =
    self.sign (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.leftEndpoint r) * x (self.coord (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.leftEndpoint r)) -
      self.sign (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.rightEndpoint r) * x (self.coord (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.rightEndpoint r))
THEOREM Trefoil.LatticeBlocks::Trefoil.LatticeBlocks.Frame.root_support
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {m : ℕ} {E : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Embedding m} (self : Trefoil.LatticeBlocks.Frame E)
  (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
  Trefoil.LatticeSupports.support (E.root r) =
    {self.coord (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.leftEndpoint r), self.coord (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.rightEndpoint r)}

THEOREM Trefoil.LatticeBlocks::Trefoil.LatticeBlocks.splitLast._proof_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (m : ℕ) (x : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Blocks m),
  (match
      match x with
      | Sum.inl i => Sum.inl (Sum.inl i)
      | Sum.inr (Sum.inl i) => Sum.inl (Sum.inr (Sum.inl i))
      | Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inl i)) => Sum.inl (Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inl i)))
      | Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inl i))) => Sum.inl (Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inr i)))
      | Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inr u))) => Sum.inr u with
    | Sum.inl (Sum.inl i) => Sum.inl i
    | Sum.inl (Sum.inr (Sum.inl i)) => Sum.inr (Sum.inl i)
    | Sum.inl (Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inl i))) => Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inl i))
    | Sum.inl (Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inr i))) => Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inl i)))
    | Sum.inr u => Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inr u)))) =
    x
THEOREM Trefoil.LatticeBlocks::Trefoil.LatticeBlocks.splitLast._proof_2
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (m : ℕ) (x : Trefoil.LatticeBlocks.Used m ⊕ Unit),
  (match
      match x with
      | Sum.inl (Sum.inl i) => Sum.inl i
      | Sum.inl (Sum.inr (Sum.inl i)) => Sum.inr (Sum.inl i)
      | Sum.inl (Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inl i))) => Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inl i))
      | Sum.inl (Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inr i))) => Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inl i)))
      | Sum.inr u => Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inr u))) with
    | Sum.inl i => Sum.inl (Sum.inl i)
    | Sum.inr (Sum.inl i) => Sum.inl (Sum.inr (Sum.inl i))
    | Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inl i)) => Sum.inl (Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inl i)))
    | Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inl i))) => Sum.inl (Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inr i)))
    | Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inr (Sum.inr u))) => Sum.inr u) =
    x
THEOREM Trefoil.LatticeArithmetic::Trefoil.LatticeConfiguration.mk.inj
AXIOMS [propext]
TYPE ∀ {m a b c z g ell rA sA tA rB sB tB rC sC tC : ℤ}
  {normA : m * a ^ 2 + 2 * a + 1 + 3 * z ^ 2 + 2 * rA ^ 2 + 2 * sA ^ 2 + tA ^ 2 = 4}
  {normB : m * b ^ 2 + (g - 1) ^ 2 + 2 * g ^ 2 + 2 * rB ^ 2 + 2 * sB ^ 2 + tB ^ 2 = 6}
  {normC : m * c ^ 2 + 2 * ell ^ 2 + (ell + 1) ^ 2 + 2 * rC ^ 2 + 2 * sC ^ 2 + tC ^ 2 = 3}
  {pairingAB : (m * a + 1) * b + z * (3 * g - 1) + 2 * (rA * rB + sA * sB) + tA * tB = -1}
  {pairingAC : (m * a + 1) * c + z * (3 * ell + 1) + 2 * (rA * rC + sA * sC) + tA * tC = 0}
  {pairingBC : m * b * c + 3 * g * ell + g - ell + 2 * (rB * rC + sB * sC) + tB * tC = 0}
  {independentB : ¬(m * b % 2 = 0 ∧ (3 * g - 1) % 2 = 0 ∧ tB % 2 = 0)}
  {a_1 b_1 c_1 z_1 g_1 ell_1 rA_1 sA_1 tA_1 rB_1 sB_1 tB_1 rC_1 sC_1 tC_1 : ℤ}
  {normA_1 : m * a_1 ^ 2 + 2 * a_1 + 1 + 3 * z_1 ^ 2 + 2 * rA_1 ^ 2 + 2 * sA_1 ^ 2 + tA_1 ^ 2 = 4}
  {normB_1 : m * b_1 ^ 2 + (g_1 - 1) ^ 2 + 2 * g_1 ^ 2 + 2 * rB_1 ^ 2 + 2 * sB_1 ^ 2 + tB_1 ^ 2 = 6}
  {normC_1 : m * c_1 ^ 2 + 2 * ell_1 ^ 2 + (ell_1 + 1) ^ 2 + 2 * rC_1 ^ 2 + 2 * sC_1 ^ 2 + tC_1 ^ 2 = 3}
  {pairingAB_1 : (m * a_1 + 1) * b_1 + z_1 * (3 * g_1 - 1) + 2 * (rA_1 * rB_1 + sA_1 * sB_1) + tA_1 * tB_1 = -1}
  {pairingAC_1 : (m * a_1 + 1) * c_1 + z_1 * (3 * ell_1 + 1) + 2 * (rA_1 * rC_1 + sA_1 * sC_1) + tA_1 * tC_1 = 0}
  {pairingBC_1 : m * b_1 * c_1 + 3 * g_1 * ell_1 + g_1 - ell_1 + 2 * (rB_1 * rC_1 + sB_1 * sC_1) + tB_1 * tC_1 = 0}
  {independentB_1 : ¬(m * b_1 % 2 = 0 ∧ (3 * g_1 - 1) % 2 = 0 ∧ tB_1 % 2 = 0)},
  { a := a, b := b, c := c, z := z, g := g, ell := ell, rA := rA, sA := sA, tA := tA, rB := rB, sB := sB, tB := tB,
        rC := rC, sC := sC, tC := tC, normA := normA, normB := normB, normC := normC, pairingAB := pairingAB,
        pairingAC := pairingAC, pairingBC := pairingBC, independentB := independentB } =
      { a := a_1, b := b_1, c := c_1, z := z_1, g := g_1, ell := ell_1, rA := rA_1, sA := sA_1, tA := tA_1, rB := rB_1,
        sB := sB_1, tB := tB_1, rC := rC_1, sC := sC_1, tC := tC_1, normA := normA_1, normB := normB_1,
        normC := normC_1, pairingAB := pairingAB_1, pairingAC := pairingAC_1, pairingBC := pairingBC_1,
        independentB := independentB_1 } →
    a = a_1 ∧
      b = b_1 ∧
        c = c_1 ∧
          z = z_1 ∧
            g = g_1 ∧
              ell = ell_1 ∧
                rA = rA_1 ∧
                  sA = sA_1 ∧ tA = tA_1 ∧ rB = rB_1 ∧ sB = sB_1 ∧ tB = tB_1 ∧ rC = rC_1 ∧ sC = sC_1 ∧ tC = tC_1

AXIOMS [propext]

        rC := rC, sC := sC, tC := tC, normA := normA, normB := normB, normC := normC, pairingAB := pairingAB,

THEOREM Trefoil.LatticeArithmetic::Trefoil.LatticeConfiguration.mk.sizeOf_spec
AXIOMS [propext]
TYPE ∀ {m : ℤ} (a b c z g ell rA sA tA rB sB tB rC sC tC : ℤ)
  (normA : m * a ^ 2 + 2 * a + 1 + 3 * z ^ 2 + 2 * rA ^ 2 + 2 * sA ^ 2 + tA ^ 2 = 4)
  (normB : m * b ^ 2 + (g - 1) ^ 2 + 2 * g ^ 2 + 2 * rB ^ 2 + 2 * sB ^ 2 + tB ^ 2 = 6)
  (normC : m * c ^ 2 + 2 * ell ^ 2 + (ell + 1) ^ 2 + 2 * rC ^ 2 + 2 * sC ^ 2 + tC ^ 2 = 3)
  (pairingAB : (m * a + 1) * b + z * (3 * g - 1) + 2 * (rA * rB + sA * sB) + tA * tB = -1)
  (pairingAC : (m * a + 1) * c + z * (3 * ell + 1) + 2 * (rA * rC + sA * sC) + tA * tC = 0)
  (pairingBC : m * b * c + 3 * g * ell + g - ell + 2 * (rB * rC + sB * sC) + tB * tC = 0)
  (independentB : ¬(m * b % 2 = 0 ∧ (3 * g - 1) % 2 = 0 ∧ tB % 2 = 0)),
  sizeOf
      { a := a, b := b, c := c, z := z, g := g, ell := ell, rA := rA, sA := sA, tA := tA, rB := rB, sB := sB, tB := tB,
        rC := rC, sC := sC, tC := tC, normA := normA, normB := normB, normC := normC, pairingAB := pairingAB,
        pairingAC := pairingAC, pairingBC := pairingBC, independentB := independentB } =
    1 + sizeOf a + sizeOf b + sizeOf c + sizeOf z + sizeOf g + sizeOf ell + sizeOf rA + sizeOf sA + sizeOf tA +
                            sizeOf rB +
                          sizeOf sB +
                        sizeOf tB +
                      sizeOf rC +
                    sizeOf sC +
                  sizeOf tC +
                sizeOf normA +
              sizeOf normB +
            sizeOf normC +
          sizeOf pairingAB +
        sizeOf pairingAC +
      sizeOf pairingBC

THEOREM Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding::Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Embedding.independent
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {m : ℕ} (self : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Embedding m),
  LinearIndependent (ZMod 2) fun r =>
    match (motive := Option (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m) → Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m → ZMod 2) r with
    | none => fun j => ↑(self.B j)
    | some i => fun j => ↑(self.root i j)

  Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.rootGram (Sum.inl i) (Sum.inl j) =
    if Sum.inl i = Sum.inl j then 2 else if ↑i + 1 = ↑j ∨ ↑j + 1 = ↑i then -1 else 0

THEOREM Trefoil.LatticeOddBasis::Trefoil.LatticeOddBasis.of_odd_basis._proof_1
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {m : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m) (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m) ℤ),
  Odd M.det →
    ∀ (choose : Option (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m) → Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m),
      Function.Injective choose →
        LinearIndependent (ZMod 2) fun r =>
          match (motive := Option (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m) → Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m → ZMod 2)
            r with
          | none => fun j => ↑(M.col (choose none) j)
          | some i => fun j => ↑(M.col (choose (some i)) j)

AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  Trefoil.LatticeParity.zeroBlockSums x =
    (∑ i, x (Sum.inl i) = 0 ∧
      ∑ i, x (Sum.inr (Sum.inl i)) = 0 ∧

AXIOMS [propext, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {K : Trefoil.Section2Topology.KnotGeometry} {A : Trefoil.Section2Topology.AugmentedGeometry K}
  {I : Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramGeometry A},
  Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramReferences I →
    ∀ (c : Trefoil.Section2TriangulationData.Certificate) (l r : A.Link),
      I.hasMarkedExterior l c.source c.sourceMarking c.sourceAxis →
        I.hasMarkedExterior r c.target c.targetMarking c.targetAxis →
          ∀
            (a :
              Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.MarkedIsomorphism c.source c.target c.tetMap c.reverse c.sourceMarking
                c.targetMarking c.peripheral),
            Trefoil.Section2TriangulationData.hasMarkedSlope c →
              A.markedExterior (Trefoil.Section2Certificate.action c.reverse) l r
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Certificate::Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramReferences.axis_chart
AXIOMS [propext, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {K : Trefoil.Section2Topology.KnotGeometry} {A : Trefoil.Section2Topology.AugmentedGeometry K}
  {I : Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramGeometry A},
  Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramReferences I →
    ∀ (b : Fin 2) (tail : List ℤ),
      (Trefoil.Arithmetic.plusPair tail).1.natAbs.Coprime (Trefoil.Arithmetic.plusPair tail).2.natAbs →
        Odd (Trefoil.Section2Arithmetic.tangleAdd (↑↑b, 1) (Trefoil.Arithmetic.plusPair tail)).1 →
          I.augmented (Trefoil.Section2ReferenceTangle.axisChartDiagram (↑↑b) tail)
              Trefoil.Section2ReferenceTangle.builtReferenceAxis =
            A.axisChart b (Trefoil.Arithmetic.plusPair tail)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Certificate::Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramReferences.marked_move
AXIOMS [propext, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {K : Trefoil.Section2Topology.KnotGeometry} {A : Trefoil.Section2Topology.AugmentedGeometry K}
  {I : Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramGeometry A},
  Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramReferences I →
    ∀ (D E : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram) (move : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Move)
      (a b : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart),
      D.markedDiagram a = true →
        E.markedDiagram b = true →
          Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.checkMove D E move = true →
            Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Move.transportAnchor a move = some b → I.augmented D a = I.augmented E b

      Odd (Trefoil.Section2Arithmetic.tangleAdd (twists, 1) (Trefoil.Arithmetic.plusPair tail)).1 →
        I.knot (Trefoil.Section2ReferenceTangle.ordinaryReference twists tail) =
          K.numerator (Trefoil.Section2Arithmetic.tangleAdd (twists, 1) (Trefoil.Arithmetic.plusPair tail))

AXIOMS [propext, Quot.sound]

  {I : Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramGeometry A},

        I.hasKnotExterior r c.target c.targetMarking →

              l = Trefoil.Section2Topology.ExteriorAction.onKnot K (Trefoil.Section2Certificate.action c.reverse) r

AXIOMS [propext, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {K : Trefoil.Section2Topology.KnotGeometry} {A : Trefoil.Section2Topology.AugmentedGeometry K}
  {I : Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramGeometry A},

      D.knotDiagram = true →
        E.knotDiagram = true → Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.checkMove D E move = true → I.knot D = I.knot E
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Certificate::Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramReferences.ordinary_split
AXIOMS [propext, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {K : Trefoil.Section2Topology.KnotGeometry} {A : Trefoil.Section2Topology.AugmentedGeometry K}
  {I : Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramGeometry A},
  Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramReferences I →
    ∀ (D : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram) (a b : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart)
      (L R : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram),
      D.knotDiagram = true →
        L.knotDiagram = true →
          R.knotDiagram = true → D.checkSplit a b none L R = true → I.knot D = K.connectedSum (I.knot L) (I.knot R)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Certificate::Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramReferences.ordinary_switch
AXIOMS [propext, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {K : Trefoil.Section2Topology.KnotGeometry} {A : Trefoil.Section2Topology.AugmentedGeometry K}
  {I : Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramGeometry A},
  Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramReferences I →
    ∀ (D E : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram) (i : ℕ)
      (p : List (Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart × Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart)),
      D.knotDiagram = true →
        E.knotDiagram = true →
          Trefoil.Section2Switches.checkSwitch D E i none none p = true → K.switch (I.knot D) (I.knot E)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Certificate::Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramReferences.ordinary_terminal
AXIOMS [propext, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {K : Trefoil.Section2Topology.KnotGeometry} {A : Trefoil.Section2Topology.AugmentedGeometry K}
  {I : Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramGeometry A},
  Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramReferences I →
    ∀ (D : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram) (trace : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace),
      Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.CertifiedMoves D trace →
        Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.withComponents D 1 trace = true →
          Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.endpoint D trace = { rows := [], circles := 1 } → I.knot D = K.unknot
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Certificate::Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramReferences.split
AXIOMS [propext, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {K : Trefoil.Section2Topology.KnotGeometry} {A : Trefoil.Section2Topology.AugmentedGeometry K}
  {I : Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramGeometry A},
  Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramReferences I →
    ∀ (D : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram) (a b c : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart)
      (L R : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram),
      D.markedDiagram c = true →
        L.knotDiagram = true →
          R.markedDiagram c = true →
            D.checkSplit a b (some c) L R = true → A.splitOff (I.augmented D c) (I.knot L) (I.augmented R c)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Certificate::Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramReferences.switch
AXIOMS [propext, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {K : Trefoil.Section2Topology.KnotGeometry} {A : Trefoil.Section2Topology.AugmentedGeometry K}
  {I : Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramGeometry A},
  Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramReferences I →
    ∀ (D E : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram) (i : ℕ) (a b : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart)
      (p : List (Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart × Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart)),
      D.markedDiagram a = true →
        E.markedDiagram b = true →
          Trefoil.Section2Switches.checkSwitch D E i (some a) (some b) p = true →
            A.crossingAway (I.augmented D a) (I.augmented E b)

AXIOMS [propext, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {K : Trefoil.Section2Topology.KnotGeometry} {A : Trefoil.Section2Topology.AugmentedGeometry K}
  {I : Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramGeometry A},
  Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramReferences I →
    ∀ (D : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram) (axis : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart)

      D.markedDiagram axis = true →
        Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.CertifiedMoves D trace →
          Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.withComponents D 2 trace = true →
            Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.endpoint D trace = { rows := [], circles := 2 } →
              A.terminalUnlink (I.augmented D axis)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Certificate::Trefoil.Section2Certificate.ExteriorBridges.source
AXIOMS []

  {I : Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramGeometry A},

    ∀ (b : Fin 2) (m : Trefoil.Section2TriangulationData.Match),
      let c := Trefoil.Section2TriangulationData.data b m;
      I.hasMarkedExterior

        c.sourceMarking c.sourceAxis

      let c := Trefoil.Section2TriangulationData.data b m;
      I.hasMarkedExterior
        (I.marked (Trefoil.Section2Certificate.rawDiagram b (Trefoil.Section2Certificate.targetRole m))) c.target
        c.targetMarking c.targetAxis

THEOREM Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves::Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.CertifiedMoves.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (D : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram) (trace : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace),
  Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.CertifiedMoves D trace =
    ((D.valid && Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.check D trace) = true)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2DiagramDomains::Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.CertifiedMoves.knotSound
AXIOMS [propext, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {α : Type u_1} (interpret : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram → α),
  (∀ (D E : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram) (move : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Move),
      D.knotDiagram = true →
        E.knotDiagram = true → Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.checkMove D E move = true → interpret D = interpret E) →
    ∀ {D : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram} {trace : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace},
      Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.CertifiedMoves D trace →
        Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.knot D trace = true →
          interpret D = interpret (Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.endpoint D trace)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2DiagramDomains::Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.CertifiedMoves.markedPlanarSound
AXIOMS [propext, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {α : Type u_1} (interpret : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram → Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart → α),
  (∀ (D E : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram) (move : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Move)
      (a b : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart),
      D.markedDiagram a = true →
        E.markedDiagram b = true →
          Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.checkMove D E move = true →
            Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Move.transportAnchor a move = some b → interpret D a = interpret E b) →
    ∀ {D : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram} {trace : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace}
      {a b : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart},
      Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.CertifiedMoves D trace →
        Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.anchor D a trace = some b →
          Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.marked D a trace = true →
            interpret D a = interpret (Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.endpoint D trace) b
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves::Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.CertifiedMoves.markedSound
AXIOMS [propext, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {α : Type u_1} (interpret : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram → Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart → α),
  (∀ (D E : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram) (move : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Move)
      (a b : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart),
      D.valid = true →
        E.valid = true →
          Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.checkMove D E move = true →
            D.darts.contains a = true →
              E.darts.contains b = true →
                Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Move.transportAnchor a move = some b → interpret D a = interpret E b) →
    ∀ {D : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram} {trace : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace}
      {a b : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart},
      Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.CertifiedMoves D trace →
        Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.anchor D a trace = some b →
          interpret D a = interpret (Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.endpoint D trace) b

THEOREM Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior::Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.build.eq_1
AXIOMS [propext]
TYPE ∀ (D : Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.SignedPD),
  Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.build D = Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.buildUsing✝ D D.incidence
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior::Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.checkSnapshot.eq_1
AXIOMS [propext]
TYPE ∀ (D : Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.SignedPD) (neighbor cuspLabels : Array ℕ) (mu lambda : Array ℤ),
  Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.checkSnapshot D neighbor cuspLabels mu lambda =
    (D.check &&
      Option.any (fun E => Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.checkBuilt✝ E neighbor cuspLabels mu lambda)
        (Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.build D))
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior::Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.givenFaces.eq_1
AXIOMS [propext]
TYPE ∀ (D : Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.SignedPD) (I : Array (List Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.HalfEdge)),
  Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.givenFaces D I =
    Array.flatMap
      (fun u =>
        Array.map Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.TetImage.tet
          (Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.octahedralFaces✝ D I (u / 4) (u % 4)))
      (Array.range (4 * D.arcs.size))

THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Pachner::Trefoil.Section2Pachner.EveryExteriorFace.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (n m : ℕ) (S T : Trefoil.Section2Pachner.Charts) (agree : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → Prop),
  Trefoil.Section2Pachner.EveryExteriorFace n m S T agree =
    ∀ (t : Fin n) (f : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Vertex),
      Trefoil.Section2Pachner.interiorFace S ↑t ↑f = false →
        ∃ u g, Trefoil.Section2Pachner.interiorFace T ↑u ↑g = false ∧ agree ↑t ↑f ↑u ↑g
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Pachner::Trefoil.Section2Pachner.ExternalAgreement.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (S T : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation) (w : Trefoil.Section2Pachner.CavityWitness),
  Trefoil.Section2Pachner.ExternalAgreement S T w =
    ((Trefoil.Section2Pachner.EveryExteriorFace (Array.size S) (Array.size T) w.beforeCharts w.afterCharts
        fun t f u g =>
        Trefoil.Section2Pachner.faceArrow S w.beforeCharts id t f =
          Trefoil.Section2Pachner.faceArrow T w.afterCharts (Trefoil.Section2Pachner.outsideId✝ w) u g) ∧
      Trefoil.Section2Pachner.EveryExteriorFace (Array.size T) (Array.size S) w.afterCharts w.beforeCharts
        fun u g t f =>
        Trefoil.Section2Pachner.faceArrow T w.afterCharts (Trefoil.Section2Pachner.outsideId✝ w) u g =
          Trefoil.Section2Pachner.faceArrow S w.beforeCharts id t f)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Pachner::Trefoil.Section2Pachner.MarkedReplacement.move

    Trefoil.Section2Pachner.CavityReplacement S.triangulation T.triangulation w side

  {side : Trefoil.Section2Pachner.Side},
  Trefoil.Section2Pachner.MarkedReplacement S T w side →
    Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.MarkingConditions T.triangulation T.marking
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Pachner::Trefoil.Section2Pachner.SameSeamFlux.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (S T : Trefoil.Section2Pachner.MarkedState) (w : Trefoil.Section2Pachner.CavityWitness),
  Trefoil.Section2Pachner.SameSeamFlux S T w =
    (S.marking.cuspCount = T.marking.cuspCount ∧
      (Trefoil.Section2Pachner.EveryExteriorFace (Array.size S.triangulation) (Array.size T.triangulation)
          w.beforeCharts w.afterCharts fun t f u g =>
          Trefoil.Section2Pachner.peripheralFace S w.beforeCharts id t f =
            Trefoil.Section2Pachner.peripheralFace T w.afterCharts (Trefoil.Section2Pachner.outsideId✝ w) u g) ∧
        Trefoil.Section2Pachner.EveryExteriorFace (Array.size T.triangulation) (Array.size S.triangulation)
          w.afterCharts w.beforeCharts fun u g t f =>
          Trefoil.Section2Pachner.peripheralFace T w.afterCharts (Trefoil.Section2Pachner.outsideId✝ w) u g =
            Trefoil.Section2Pachner.peripheralFace S w.beforeCharts id t f)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Pachner::Trefoil.Section2Pachner.Side.ofNat_toCtorIdx
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (x : Trefoil.Section2Pachner.Side), Trefoil.Section2Pachner.Side.ofNat x.toCtorIdx = x
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Pachner::Trefoil.Section2Pachner.SourceSurvivor.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (T : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation) (w : Trefoil.Section2Pachner.CavityWitness) (t : ℕ),
  Trefoil.Section2Pachner.SourceSurvivor T w t =
    match w.oldToNew.getD t none with
    | none => Trefoil.Section2Pachner.survives w.beforeCharts t = false
    | some u =>
      Trefoil.Section2Pachner.survives w.beforeCharts t = true ∧
        u < Array.size T ∧ Trefoil.Section2Pachner.survives w.afterCharts u = true ∧ w.newToOld.getD u none = some t
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Pachner::Trefoil.Section2Pachner.SurvivingTetrahedra.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (S T : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation) (w : Trefoil.Section2Pachner.CavityWitness),
  Trefoil.Section2Pachner.SurvivingTetrahedra S T w =
    (w.oldToNew.size = Array.size S ∧
      w.newToOld.size = Array.size T ∧
        (∀ (t : Fin (Array.size S)), Trefoil.Section2Pachner.SourceSurvivor T w ↑t) ∧
          ∀ (u : Fin (Array.size T)), Trefoil.Section2Pachner.TargetSurvivor S w ↑u)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Pachner::Trefoil.Section2Pachner.TargetSurvivor.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (S : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation) (w : Trefoil.Section2Pachner.CavityWitness) (u : ℕ),
  Trefoil.Section2Pachner.TargetSurvivor S w u =
    match w.newToOld.getD u none with
    | none => Trefoil.Section2Pachner.survives w.afterCharts u = false
    | some t =>
      Trefoil.Section2Pachner.survives w.afterCharts u = true ∧
        t < Array.size S ∧ Trefoil.Section2Pachner.survives w.beforeCharts t = true ∧ w.oldToNew.getD t none = some u
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Pachner::Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkExternal.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (S T : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation) (w : Trefoil.Section2Pachner.CavityWitness),
  Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkExternal S T w =
    ((Trefoil.Section2Pachner.matchedFaces✝ (Array.size S) (Array.size T) w.beforeCharts w.afterCharts w.oldToNew
        fun t f u g =>
        decide
          (Trefoil.Section2Pachner.faceArrow S w.beforeCharts id t f =
            Trefoil.Section2Pachner.faceArrow T w.afterCharts (Trefoil.Section2Pachner.outsideId✝ w) u g)) &&
      Trefoil.Section2Pachner.matchedFaces✝ (Array.size T) (Array.size S) w.afterCharts w.beforeCharts w.newToOld
        fun u g t f =>
        decide
          (Trefoil.Section2Pachner.faceArrow T w.afterCharts (Trefoil.Section2Pachner.outsideId✝ w) u g =
            Trefoil.Section2Pachner.faceArrow S w.beforeCharts id t f))
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Pachner::Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkInternal_sound._simp_1_1
AXIOMS [propext, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {n : ℕ} {f : Fin n → Bool}, (Trefoil.Section2Pachner.allP✝ n f = true) = ∀ (i : Fin n), f i = true
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Pachner::Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkInternal_sound._simp_1_2
AXIOMS [propext]
TYPE ∀ {a b c : Prop}, ((a ∧ b) ∧ c) = (a ∧ b ∧ c)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Pachner::Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkMarkedMove.eq_1
AXIOMS [propext]
TYPE ∀ (S T : Trefoil.Section2Pachner.MarkedState) (w : Trefoil.Section2Pachner.CavityWitness)
  (side : Trefoil.Section2Pachner.Side),
  Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkMarkedMove S T w side =
    (Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkMove S.triangulation T.triangulation w side &&
          Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkCuspMarking S.triangulation S.marking &&
        Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkCuspMarking T.triangulation T.marking &&
      Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkSeamFlux S T w)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Pachner::Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkMove.eq_1
AXIOMS [propext]
TYPE ∀ (S T : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation) (w : Trefoil.Section2Pachner.CavityWitness)
  (side : Trefoil.Section2Pachner.Side),
  Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkMove S T w side =
    (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkTriangulation S && Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkTriangulation T &&
                  Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkSite S w.beforeCharts side &&
                Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkSite T w.afterCharts side.other &&
              Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkSurvivors S T w &&
            Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkInternal S w.beforeCharts &&
          Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkInternal T w.afterCharts &&
        Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkExternal S T w &&
      Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkPolarity w)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Pachner::Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkPolarity.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (w : Trefoil.Section2Pachner.CavityWitness),
  Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkPolarity w =
    (((Trefoil.Section2Pachner.polarityList w).all fun x => x == true) ||
      (Trefoil.Section2Pachner.polarityList w).all fun x => x == false)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Pachner::Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkSeamFlux.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (S T : Trefoil.Section2Pachner.MarkedState) (w : Trefoil.Section2Pachner.CavityWitness),
  Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkSeamFlux S T w =
    ((decide (S.marking.cuspCount = T.marking.cuspCount) &&
        Trefoil.Section2Pachner.matchedFaces✝ (Array.size S.triangulation) (Array.size T.triangulation) w.beforeCharts
          w.afterCharts w.oldToNew fun t f u g =>
          decide
            (Trefoil.Section2Pachner.peripheralFace S w.beforeCharts id t f =
              Trefoil.Section2Pachner.peripheralFace T w.afterCharts (Trefoil.Section2Pachner.outsideId✝ w) u g)) &&
      Trefoil.Section2Pachner.matchedFaces✝ (Array.size T.triangulation) (Array.size S.triangulation) w.afterCharts
        w.beforeCharts w.newToOld fun u g t f =>
        decide
          (Trefoil.Section2Pachner.peripheralFace T w.afterCharts (Trefoil.Section2Pachner.outsideId✝ w) u g =
            Trefoil.Section2Pachner.peripheralFace S w.beforeCharts id t f))
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Pachner::Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkSite.eq_1
AXIOMS [propext]
TYPE ∀ (S : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation) (charts : Trefoil.Section2Pachner.Charts)
  (side : Trefoil.Section2Pachner.Side),
  Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkSite S charts side =
    (decide (Array.size charts = Array.size S) &&
      decide
        (Trefoil.Section2Pachner.sortBy
            (fun a b => decide (Trefoil.Section2Pachner.chartKey✝ a ≤ Trefoil.Section2Pachner.chartKey✝ b))
            (Trefoil.Section2Pachner.sortedSites charts) =
          Trefoil.Section2Pachner.sortBy
            (fun a b => decide (Trefoil.Section2Pachner.chartKey✝ a ≤ Trefoil.Section2Pachner.chartKey✝ b))
            side.simplices))
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Pachner::Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkSourceSurvivor.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (T : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation) (w : Trefoil.Section2Pachner.CavityWitness) (t : ℕ),
  Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkSourceSurvivor T w t =
    match w.oldToNew.getD t none with
    | none => !Trefoil.Section2Pachner.survives w.beforeCharts t
    | some u =>
      Trefoil.Section2Pachner.survives w.beforeCharts t && decide (u < Array.size T) &&
          Trefoil.Section2Pachner.survives w.afterCharts u &&
        decide (w.newToOld.getD u none = some t)

        Trefoil.Section2Pachner.allP✝ (Array.size S) fun t => Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkSourceSurvivor T w ↑t) &&
      Trefoil.Section2Pachner.allP✝ (Array.size T) fun t => Trefoil.Section2Pachner.checkTargetSurvivor S w ↑t)

      tail.1.natAbs.Coprime tail.2.natAbs →
        Odd (Trefoil.Section2Arithmetic.tangleAdd (↑(Trefoil.Section2Arithmetic.halfTwists b t), 1) tail).1 →

          Trefoil.Section2Topology.ExteriorAction.onKnot K action
            (A.fill e (action.onSlope (Trefoil.Section2Arithmetic.twistSlope t)))

      A.splitOff d k e →
        A.fill d (Trefoil.Section2Arithmetic.twistSlope t) =

THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Triangulations::Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.ClosedCurve.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (T : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation) (curve : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.SignedTable),
  Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.ClosedCurve T curve =
    (curve.size = 16 * Array.size T ∧
      ∀ (t : Fin (Array.size T)) (v : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Vertex),
        Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry curve ↑t ↑v ↑v = 0 ∧
          Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry curve (↑t) (↑v) 0 +
                    Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry curve (↑t) (↑v) 1 +
                  Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry curve (↑t) (↑v) 2 +
                Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry curve (↑t) (↑v) 3 =
              0 ∧
            ∀ (f : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Vertex),
              f ≠ v →
                have g := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.face T ↑t ↑f;
                Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry curve ↑t ↑v ↑f =
                  -Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry curve g.tet (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex g ↑v)
                      (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex g ↑f))
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Triangulations::Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.GluedPotential.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (T : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation) (P : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.SignedTable),
  Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.GluedPotential T P =
    (P.size = 16 * Array.size T ∧
      ∀ (t : Fin (Array.size T)) (v f w : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Vertex),
        f ≠ v →
          f ≠ w →
            v ≠ w →
              have g := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.face T ↑t ↑f;
              Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry P ↑t ↑v ↑w =
                Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry P g.tet (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex g ↑v)
                  (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex g ↑w))
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Triangulations::Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.IsoConditions.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (S T : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation)
  (certificate : Array Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.TetImage) (reverse : Bool),
  Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.IsoConditions S T certificate reverse =
    (certificate.size = Array.size S ∧
      Array.size S = Array.size T ∧
        (∀ (t u : Fin (Array.size S)),
            (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.image certificate ↑t).tet =
                (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.image certificate ↑u).tet →
              t = u) ∧
          ∀ (t : Fin (Array.size S)),
            have p := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.image certificate ↑t;
            p.tet < Array.size T ∧
              Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.VertexPermutation p ∧
                Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.oddVertices p = reverse ∧
                  ∀ (f : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Vertex),
                    have a := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.face S ↑t ↑f;
                    have b := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.face T p.tet (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex p ↑f);
                    have q := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.image certificate a.tet;
                    q.tet = b.tet ∧
                      ∀ (v : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Vertex),
                        Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex q (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex a ↑v) =
                          Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex b (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex p ↑v))

THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Triangulations::Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.MarkingConditions.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (T : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation) (M : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.CuspMarking),
  Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.MarkingConditions T M =
    (0 < M.cuspCount ∧
      M.cuspIndices.size = 4 * Array.size T ∧
        (∀ (c : Fin M.cuspCount), ∃ t v, Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.cusp M ↑t ↑v = ↑c) ∧
          (∀ (t : Fin (Array.size T)) (v : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Vertex),
              Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.cusp M ↑t ↑v < M.cuspCount ∧
                ∀ (f : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Vertex),
                  f ≠ v →
                    have g := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.face T ↑t ↑f;
                    Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.cusp M ↑t ↑v =
                      Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.cusp M g.tet (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex g ↑v)) ∧
            Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.ClosedCurve T M.meridian ∧
              Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.ClosedCurve T M.longitude)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Triangulations::Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.PeripheralConditions.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (S T : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation)
  (certificate : Array Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.TetImage) (MS MT : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.CuspMarking)
  (w : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.PeripheralWitness),
  Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.PeripheralConditions S T certificate MS MT w =
    (w.maps.size = MS.cuspCount ∧
      MS.cuspCount = MT.cuspCount ∧
        (∀ (c : Fin MS.cuspCount), (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.peripheralImage w ↑c).cusp < MT.cuspCount) ∧
          (∀ (c d : Fin MS.cuspCount),
              (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.peripheralImage w ↑c).cusp =
                  (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.peripheralImage w ↑d).cusp →
                c = d) ∧
            Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.GluedPotential T w.meridianPotential ∧
              Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.GluedPotential T w.longitudePotential ∧
                ∀ (t : Fin (Array.size S)) (v : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Vertex),
                  have p := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.image certificate ↑t;
                  have dest :=
                    Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.peripheralImage w (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.cusp MS ↑t ↑v);
                  have u := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex p ↑v;
                  Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.cusp MT p.tet u = dest.cusp ∧
                    ∀ (f : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Vertex),
                      f ≠ v →
                        have g := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex p ↑f;
                        have targetMu := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry MT.meridian p.tet u g;
                        have targetLambda := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry MT.longitude p.tet u g;
                        Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry MS.meridian ↑t ↑v ↑f -
                              (dest.mm * targetMu + dest.lm * targetLambda) =
                            Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.boundaryEntry w.meridianPotential p.tet u g ∧
                          Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry MS.longitude ↑t ↑v ↑f -
                              (dest.ml * targetMu + dest.ll * targetLambda) =
                            Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.boundaryEntry w.longitudePotential p.tet u g)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Triangulations::Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Valid.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (T : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation),
  Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Valid T =
    (0 < Array.size T ∧
      ∀ (t : Fin (Array.size T)),
        (Array.getD T ↑t #[]).size = 4 ∧
          ∀ (f : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Vertex),
            have a := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.face T ↑t ↑f;
            have b := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.face T a.tet (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex a ↑f);
            a.tet < Array.size T ∧
              Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.VertexPermutation a ∧
                Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.oddVertices a = true ∧
                  (a.tet ≠ ↑t ∨ Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex a ↑f ≠ ↑f) ∧
                    b.tet = ↑t ∧
                      ∀ (v : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Vertex),
                        Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex b (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex a ↑v) = ↑v)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Triangulations::Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.VertexPermutation.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (a : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.TetImage),
  Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.VertexPermutation a =
    (a.vertices.size = 4 ∧
      (∀ (v : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Vertex), Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex a ↑v < 4) ∧
        ∀ (v w : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Vertex),
          Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex a ↑v = Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex a ↑w → v = w)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Triangulations::Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkCuspMarking.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (T : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation) (M : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.CuspMarking),
  Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkCuspMarking T M =
    (((decide (0 < M.cuspCount) && decide (M.cuspIndices.size = 4 * Array.size T) &&
            Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ M.cuspCount fun c =>
              Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.anyFin✝ (Array.size T) fun t =>
                Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.anyFin✝ 4 fun v =>
                  decide (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.cusp M ↑t ↑v = ↑c)) &&
          Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ (Array.size T) fun t =>
            Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ 4 fun v =>
              decide (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.cusp M ↑t ↑v < M.cuspCount) &&
                Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ 4 fun f =>
                  decide
                    (f ≠ v →
                      have g := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.face T ↑t ↑f;
                      Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.cusp M ↑t ↑v =
                        Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.cusp M g.tet (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex g ↑v))) &&
        Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkClosedCurve✝ T M.meridian &&
      Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkClosedCurve✝ T M.longitude)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Triangulations::Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkCuspMarking_sound._simp_1_3
AXIOMS [propext, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {n : ℕ} {test : Fin n → Bool},
  (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ n test = true) = ∀ (i : Fin n), test i = true
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Triangulations::Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkCuspMarking_sound._simp_1_4
AXIOMS [propext, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {n : ℕ} {test : Fin n → Bool}, (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.anyFin✝ n test = true) = ∃ i, test i = true
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Triangulations::Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkCuspMarking_sound._simp_1_5

THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Triangulations::Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkIso.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (S T : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation)
  (certificate : Array Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.TetImage) (reverse : Bool),
  Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkIso S T certificate reverse =
    ((decide (certificate.size = Array.size S) && decide (Array.size S = Array.size T) &&
        Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ (Array.size S) fun t =>
          Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ (Array.size S) fun u =>
            decide
              ((Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.image certificate ↑t).tet =
                  (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.image certificate ↑u).tet →
                t = u)) &&
      Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ (Array.size S) fun t =>
        have p := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.image certificate ↑t;
        decide (p.tet < Array.size T) && Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkVertexPermutation✝ p &&
            decide (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.oddVertices p = reverse) &&
          Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ 4 fun f =>
            have a := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.face S ↑t ↑f;
            have b := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.face T p.tet (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex p ↑f);
            have q := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.image certificate a.tet;
            decide (q.tet = b.tet) &&
              Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ 4 fun v =>
                decide
                  (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex q (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex a ↑v) =
                    Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex b (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex p ↑v)))
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Triangulations::Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkIso_conditions._simp_1_3

THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Triangulations::Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkIso_sound._proof_4

      Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.VertexPermutation (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.image c ↑t)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Triangulations::Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkIso_sound._proof_6
AXIOMS [Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  (hi : Function.Injective fun t => ⟨(Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.image c ↑t).tet, ⋯⟩)

THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Triangulations::Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkPeripheral.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (S T : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation)
  (certificate : Array Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.TetImage) (MS MT : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.CuspMarking)
  (w : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.PeripheralWitness),
  Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkPeripheral S T certificate MS MT w =
    (((decide (w.maps.size = MS.cuspCount) && decide (MS.cuspCount = MT.cuspCount) &&
              Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ MS.cuspCount fun c =>
                decide ((Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.peripheralImage w ↑c).cusp < MT.cuspCount)) &&
            Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ MS.cuspCount fun c =>
              Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ MS.cuspCount fun d =>
                decide
                  ((Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.peripheralImage w ↑c).cusp =
                      (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.peripheralImage w ↑d).cusp →
                    c = d)) &&
          Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkGluedPotential✝ T w.meridianPotential &&
        Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkGluedPotential✝ T w.longitudePotential &&
      Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ (Array.size S) fun t =>
        Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ 4 fun v =>
          have p := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.image certificate ↑t;
          have dest := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.peripheralImage w (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.cusp MS ↑t ↑v);
          have u := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex p ↑v;
          decide (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.cusp MT p.tet u = dest.cusp) &&
            Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ 4 fun f =>
              decide
                (f ≠ v →
                  have g := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex p ↑f;
                  have targetMu := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry MT.meridian p.tet u g;
                  have targetLambda := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry MT.longitude p.tet u g;
                  Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry MS.meridian ↑t ↑v ↑f -
                        (dest.mm * targetMu + dest.lm * targetLambda) =
                      Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.boundaryEntry w.meridianPotential p.tet u g ∧
                    Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry MS.longitude ↑t ↑v ↑f -
                        (dest.ml * targetMu + dest.ll * targetLambda) =
                      Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.boundaryEntry w.longitudePotential p.tet u g))
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Triangulations::Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkPeripheral_sound._simp_1_3

THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Triangulations::Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkTriangulation.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (T : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation),
  Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkTriangulation T =
    (decide (0 < Array.size T) &&
      Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ (Array.size T) fun t =>
        decide ((Array.getD T ↑t #[]).size = 4) &&
          Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ 4 fun f =>
            have a := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.face T ↑t ↑f;
            have b := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.face T a.tet (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex a ↑f);
            decide (a.tet < Array.size T) && Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkVertexPermutation✝ a &&
                    Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.oddVertices a &&
                  decide (a.tet ≠ ↑t ∨ Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex a ↑f ≠ ↑f) &&
                decide (b.tet = ↑t) &&
              Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ 4 fun v =>
                decide (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex b (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex a ↑v) = ↑v))

THEOREM Trefoil.Section3ASignature::Trefoil.Section3ASignature.SignatureReferences.chainSignature_eq_signedPivotCount
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {Knot : Type u_1} {twoBridge : ℕ → ℕ → Knot} {signature : Knot → ℤ}
  (self : Trefoil.Section3ASignature.SignatureReferences twoBridge signature) (weights : List ℤ),
  (∀
      pivot ∈
        Trefoil.Arithmetic.rightPivots
          (List.map (fun w => w) do
            let a ← weights
            pure ↑a),
      pivot ≠ 0) →
    self.chainSignature weights =
      Trefoil.Arithmetic.signedPivotCount
        (Trefoil.Arithmetic.rightPivots
          (List.map (fun w => w) do
            let a ← weights
            pure ↑a))
THEOREM Trefoil.Section3ASignature::Trefoil.Section3ASignature.SignatureReferences.evenPlumbingSignature
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {Knot : Type u_1} {twoBridge : ℕ → ℕ → Knot} {signature : Knot → ℤ}
  (self : Trefoil.Section3ASignature.SignatureReferences twoBridge signature) (p q : ℕ) (weights : List ℤ),
  0 < q →
    q < p →
      p.Coprime q →
        Odd p →
          (∀ w ∈ weights, Even w) →
            Trefoil.Section3ASignature.MatchesFraction weights p q →
              signature (twoBridge p q) = self.chainSignature weights

AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

  ((T.intersection H.X) x) y =

AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

THEOREM Trefoil.Section3ATopology::Trefoil.Section3ATopology.References.chain_plumbing
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {T : Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Geometry} (self : Trefoil.Section3ATopology.References T) (weights : List ℤ)
  (p q : ℕ),
  Trefoil.Arithmetic.minusPair weights = (↑p, ↑q) →
    0 < q →
      q < p →
        p.Coprime q →
          ∃ G,
            T.boundary G.X = T.lens p q ∧
              ∀ (i j : Fin weights.length),
                ((T.intersection G.X) (G.handle i)) (G.handle j) = Trefoil.Section3APlumbing.chainGram weights i j

AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

    (∀ a ∈ weights, 0 < a) →
      Trefoil.Arithmetic.plusPair weights = (↑p, ↑q) →
        Odd p → q < p → p.Coprime q → T.conway weights = T.twoBridge p q ∨ T.mirror (T.conway weights) = T.twoBridge p q

THEOREM Trefoil.Section3ATopology::Trefoil.Section3ATopology.References.owens
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {T : Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Geometry} (self : Trefoil.Section3ATopology.References T) (K : T.Knot)
  (pos neg : ℕ),
  T.crossingChanges K pos neg →
    T.signature K = 2 * ↑neg →
      ∃ W,
        T.boundary W.X = T.doubleCover K ∧
          Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Positive T W.X ∧
            (Matrix.det fun i j => ((T.intersection W.X) (W.handle i)) (W.handle j)) =
                ↑(Nat.card (T.H1BoundaryZ (T.doubleCover K)).carrier) ∧
              ∀ (i j : Fin (pos + neg)),
                ((T.intersection W.X) (W.handle (Trefoil.Section3ATopology.meridian i)))
                    (W.handle (Trefoil.Section3ATopology.meridian j)) =
                  if i = j then 2 else 0

THEOREM Trefoil.Section3BTopology::Trefoil.Section3BTopology.FloerReferences.goeritz_sharp
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {T : Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Geometry} (self : Trefoil.Section3BTopology.FloerReferences T) (K : T.Knot)
  (p q : ℕ),
  self.alternating K →
    T.doubleCover K = T.lens p q →
      Odd p →
        0 < q →
          q < p →
            p.Coprime q →
              ∀ (j : ZMod p), self.goeritzMinimum K p (p - q) j = self.correction (T.doubleCover K) p (p - q) j
THEOREM Trefoil.Section3BTopology::Trefoil.Section3BTopology.FloerReferences.osz_negative_lens
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {T : Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Geometry} (self : Trefoil.Section3BTopology.FloerReferences T) (p t : ℕ),
  Odd p →
    0 < t →
      t < p →
        p.Coprime t →
          ∀ (i : Fin p),
            self.correction (T.reverse (T.lens p t)) p t (self.rawLensLabel p t i) =
              Trefoil.Finite.correctionTerm p t ↑i
THEOREM Trefoil.Section3BTopology::Trefoil.Section3BTopology.FloerReferences.owens_alternating
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {T : Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Geometry} (self : Trefoil.Section3BTopology.FloerReferences T) (K : T.Knot)
  (p q : ℕ),
  self.alternating K →
    T.signature K = 4 →
      T.crossingChanges K 0 2 →
        T.doubleCover K = T.lens p q →
          Odd p →
            0 < q →
              q < p →
                p.Coprime q →
                  ∃ h a s,
                    0 ≤ a ∧
                      a < h ∧
                        h ≤ s ∧
                          Even h ∧
                            Even s ∧
                              (Trefoil.Section3BMinimum.surgeryMatrixZ h a s).det = ↑p ∧
                                Nonempty
                                  (Trefoil.Section3BMinimum.OwensMinimum p h a s (self.goeritzMinimum K p (p - q)))

THEOREM Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior::_private.Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.0.Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.buildUsing.eq_1
AXIOMS [propext]
TYPE ∀ (D : Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.SignedPD) (I : Array (List Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.HalfEdge)),
  Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.buildUsing✝ D I =
    Option.map
      (fun meridian =>
        have tets :=
          Array.map (fun u => Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.octahedralFaces✝ D I (u / 4) (u % 4))
            (Array.range (4 * D.arcs.size));
        have cusps := Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.givenCusps D;
        have adjusted :=
          Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.correctLongitudes meridian (Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.givenParallel D)
            cusps (Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.givenWrithe D);
        { tetrahedra := tets,
          cusps :=
            { cuspCount := 2 + D.componentSizes.size, cuspIndices := cusps,
              meridian := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.SignedTable.array meridian,
              longitude := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.SignedTable.array adjusted } })
      (Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.givenMeridians D I)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior::_private.Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.0.Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.checkBuilt.eq_1
AXIOMS [propext]
TYPE ∀ (E : Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.InitialExterior) (neighbor cuspLabels : Array ℕ) (mu lambda : Array ℤ),
  Trefoil.Section2InitialExterior.checkBuilt✝ E neighbor cuspLabels mu lambda =
    ((Array.flatMap (fun row => Array.map Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.TetImage.tet row) E.tetrahedra == neighbor &&
          E.cusps.cuspIndices == cuspLabels &&
        match E.cusps.meridian with
        | Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.SignedTable.array a => a == mu
        | Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.SignedTable.packed size width word => false) &&
      match E.cusps.longitude with
      | Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.SignedTable.array a => a == lambda
      | Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.SignedTable.packed size width word => false)

THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Triangulations::_private.Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.0.Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkClosedCurve.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (T : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation) (curve : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.SignedTable),
  Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkClosedCurve✝ T curve =
    (decide (curve.size = 16 * Array.size T) &&
      Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ (Array.size T) fun t =>
        Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ 4 fun v =>
          decide (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry curve ↑t ↑v ↑v = 0) &&
              decide
                (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry curve (↑t) (↑v) 0 +
                        Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry curve (↑t) (↑v) 1 +
                      Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry curve (↑t) (↑v) 2 +
                    Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry curve (↑t) (↑v) 3 =
                  0) &&
            Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ 4 fun f =>
              decide
                (f ≠ v →
                  have g := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.face T ↑t ↑f;
                  Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry curve ↑t ↑v ↑f =
                    -Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry curve g.tet (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex g ↑v)
                        (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex g ↑f)))
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Triangulations::_private.Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.0.Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkClosedCurve_true._simp_1_3

  (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ n test = true) = ∀ (i : Fin n), test i = true

THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Triangulations::_private.Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.0.Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkGluedPotential.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (T : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation) (P : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.SignedTable),
  Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.checkGluedPotential✝ T P =
    (decide (P.size = 16 * Array.size T) &&
      Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ (Array.size T) fun t =>
        Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ 4 fun v =>
          Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ 4 fun f =>
            Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ 4 fun w =>
              decide
                (f ≠ v →
                  f ≠ w →
                    v ≠ w →
                      have g := Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.face T ↑t ↑f;
                      Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry P ↑t ↑v ↑w =
                        Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.entry P g.tet (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex g ↑v)
                          (Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.vertex g ↑w)))

      Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ 4 fun v =>
        Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.allFin✝ 4 fun w =>

        ((Trefoil.Finite.surgeryMatrix h a s).mulVec z = fun i => ↑(x i)) →

THEOREM Trefoil.FiniteCorrectionTerms::Trefoil.Finite.correctionTerm._unary._proof_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE WellFounded (invImage (fun x => PSigma.casesOn x fun p q => PSigma.casesOn q fun q i => q) sizeOfWFRel).1
THEOREM Trefoil.FiniteCorrectionTerms::Trefoil.Finite.correctionTerm._unary._proof_2
AXIOMS [propext]
TYPE ∀ (p q i : ℕ),
  ¬q = 0 →
    (invImage (fun x => PSigma.casesOn x fun p q => PSigma.casesOn q fun q i => q) sizeOfWFRel).1 ⟨q, ⟨p % q, i % q⟩⟩
      ⟨p, ⟨q, i⟩⟩
THEOREM Trefoil.FiniteCorrectionTerms::Trefoil.Finite.correctionTerm._unary.eq_def
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ (_x : (_ : ℕ) ×' (_ : ℕ) ×' ℕ),
  Trefoil.Finite.correctionTerm._unary _x =
    PSigma.casesOn _x fun p q =>
      PSigma.casesOn q fun q i =>
        if hq : q = 0 then 0
        else
          (↑p * ↑q - (2 * ↑i + 1 - ↑p - ↑q) ^ 2) / (4 * ↑p * ↑q) -
            Trefoil.Finite.correctionTerm._unary ⟨q, ⟨p % q, i % q⟩⟩
THEOREM Trefoil.LatticeBlocks::Trefoil.LatticeBlocks.Frame.mk.inj
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {m : ℕ} {E : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Embedding m}
  {coord : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Blocks m ≃ Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m}
  {sign : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Blocks m → ℤ} {sign_sq : ∀ (j : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Blocks m), sign j ^ 2 = 1}
  {root_support :
    ∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.support (E.root r) =
        {coord (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.leftEndpoint r), coord (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.rightEndpoint r)}}
  {pairing :
    ∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m) (x : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m → ℤ),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot x (E.root r) =
        sign (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.leftEndpoint r) * x (coord (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.leftEndpoint r)) -
          sign (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.rightEndpoint r) * x (coord (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.rightEndpoint r))}
  {coord_1 : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Blocks m ≃ Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m}
  {sign_1 : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Blocks m → ℤ}
  {sign_sq_1 : ∀ (j : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Blocks m), sign_1 j ^ 2 = 1}
  {root_support_1 :
    ∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.support (E.root r) =
        {coord_1 (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.leftEndpoint r), coord_1 (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.rightEndpoint r)}}
  {pairing_1 :
    ∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m) (x : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m → ℤ),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot x (E.root r) =
        sign_1 (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.leftEndpoint r) * x (coord_1 (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.leftEndpoint r)) -
          sign_1 (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.rightEndpoint r) * x (coord_1 (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.rightEndpoint r))},
  { coord := coord, sign := sign, sign_sq := sign_sq, root_support := root_support, pairing := pairing } =
      { coord := coord_1, sign := sign_1, sign_sq := sign_sq_1, root_support := root_support_1, pairing := pairing_1 } →
    coord = coord_1 ∧ sign = sign_1

THEOREM Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding::Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Embedding.mk.inj
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {m : ℕ} {root : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m → Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Vector m}
  {A B C : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Vector m}
  {root_pairing :
    ∀ (r s : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot (root r) (root s) = Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.rootGram r s}
  {normA : ∑ j, A j ^ 2 = 4} {normB : ∑ j, B j ^ 2 = 6} {normC : ∑ j, C j ^ 2 = 3}
  {pairingAB : Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot A B = -1} {pairingAC : Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot A C = 0}
  {pairingBC : Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot B C = 0}
  {pairingA_root :
    ∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot A (root r) = Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.aRootPairing r}
  {pairingB_root :
    ∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot B (root r) = Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.bRootPairing r}
  {pairingC_root :
    ∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot C (root r) = Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.cRootPairing r}
  {independent :
    LinearIndependent (ZMod 2) fun r =>
      match (motive := Option (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m) → Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m → ZMod 2)
        r with
      | none => fun j => ↑(B j)
      | some i => fun j => ↑(root i j)}
  {root_1 : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m → Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Vector m}
  {A_1 B_1 C_1 : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Vector m}
  {root_pairing_1 :
    ∀ (r s : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot (root_1 r) (root_1 s) = Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.rootGram r s}
  {normA_1 : ∑ j, A_1 j ^ 2 = 4} {normB_1 : ∑ j, B_1 j ^ 2 = 6} {normC_1 : ∑ j, C_1 j ^ 2 = 3}
  {pairingAB_1 : Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot A_1 B_1 = -1} {pairingAC_1 : Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot A_1 C_1 = 0}
  {pairingBC_1 : Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot B_1 C_1 = 0}
  {pairingA_root_1 :
    ∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot A_1 (root_1 r) = Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.aRootPairing r}
  {pairingB_root_1 :
    ∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot B_1 (root_1 r) = Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.bRootPairing r}
  {pairingC_root_1 :
    ∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot C_1 (root_1 r) = Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.cRootPairing r}
  {independent_1 :
    LinearIndependent (ZMod 2) fun r =>
      match (motive := Option (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m) → Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m → ZMod 2)
        r with
      | none => fun j => ↑(B_1 j)
      | some i => fun j => ↑(root_1 i j)},
  { root := root, A := A, B := B, C := C, root_pairing := root_pairing, normA := normA, normB := normB, normC := normC,
        pairingAB := pairingAB, pairingAC := pairingAC, pairingBC := pairingBC, pairingA_root := pairingA_root,
        pairingB_root := pairingB_root, pairingC_root := pairingC_root, independent := independent } =
      { root := root_1, A := A_1, B := B_1, C := C_1, root_pairing := root_pairing_1, normA := normA_1,
        normB := normB_1, normC := normC_1, pairingAB := pairingAB_1, pairingAC := pairingAC_1,
        pairingBC := pairingBC_1, pairingA_root := pairingA_root_1, pairingB_root := pairingB_root_1,
        pairingC_root := pairingC_root_1, independent := independent_1 } →
    root = root_1 ∧ A = A_1 ∧ B = B_1 ∧ C = C_1

        diagonal_coordinates := diagonal_coordinates, naturality := naturality } =
      { root := root_1, A := A_1, Bheavy := Bheavy_1, C := C_1, root_pairing := root_pairing_1, normA := normA_1,
        normB := normB_1, normC := normC_1, pairingAB := pairingAB_1, pairingAC := pairingAC_1,
        pairingBC := pairingBC_1, pairingA_root := pairingA_root_1, pairingB_root := pairingB_root_1,
        pairingC_root := pairingC_root_1, boundary := boundary_1, inclusion := inclusion_1,
        exact_at_pieces := exact_at_pieces_1, diagonal_coordinates := diagonal_coordinates_1,
        naturality := naturality_1 } →
    root = root_1 ∧
      A = A_1 ∧
        Bheavy = Bheavy_1 ∧
          C = C_1 ∧ boundary = boundary_1 ∧ inclusion = inclusion_1 ∧ diagonal_coordinates = diagonal_coordinates_1

TYPE ∀ {m : ℕ} {X : Type u_2} [inst : AddCommGroup X] [inst_1 : _root_.Module (ZMod 2) X]
  (root : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m → Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Vector m)
  (A Bheavy C : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Vector m)
  (root_pairing :
    ∀ (r s : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot (root r) (root s) = Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.rootGram r s)
  (normA : ∑ j, A j ^ 2 = 4) (normB : ∑ j, Bheavy j ^ 2 = 6) (normC : ∑ j, C j ^ 2 = 3)
  (pairingAB : Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot A Bheavy = -1) (pairingAC : Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot A C = 0)
  (pairingBC : Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot Bheavy C = 0)
  (pairingA_root :
    ∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot A (root r) = Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.aRootPairing r)
  (pairingB_root :
    ∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot Bheavy (root r) = Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.bRootPairing r)
  (pairingC_root :
    ∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot C (root r) = Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.cRootPairing r)
  (boundary : Trefoil.RelativeTopology.lensTwoCycles m →ₗ[ZMod 2] Trefoil.RelativeTopology.pieceHandleModule m)
  (inclusion : Trefoil.RelativeTopology.pieceHandleModule m →ₗ[ZMod 2] X)
  (exact_at_pieces : ∀ (z : Trefoil.RelativeTopology.pieceHandleModule m), inclusion z = 0 → ∃ b, boundary b = z)
  (diagonal_coordinates : X ≃ₗ[ZMod 2] Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m → ZMod 2)
  (naturality :
    ∀ (r : Option (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m)) (j : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m),
      diagonal_coordinates (inclusion ((Trefoil.RelativeTopology.canonicalSelectedHandles m) (Finsupp.single r 1))) j =
        match r with
        | none => ↑(Bheavy j)
        | some i => ↑(root i j))
  (root_1 : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m → Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Vector m)
  (A_1 Bheavy_1 C_1 : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Vector m)
  (root_pairing_1 :
    ∀ (r s : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot (root_1 r) (root_1 s) = Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.rootGram r s)
  (normA_1 : ∑ j, A_1 j ^ 2 = 4) (normB_1 : ∑ j, Bheavy_1 j ^ 2 = 6) (normC_1 : ∑ j, C_1 j ^ 2 = 3)
  (pairingAB_1 : Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot A_1 Bheavy_1 = -1) (pairingAC_1 : Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot A_1 C_1 = 0)
  (pairingBC_1 : Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot Bheavy_1 C_1 = 0)
  (pairingA_root_1 :
    ∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot A_1 (root_1 r) = Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.aRootPairing r)
  (pairingB_root_1 :
    ∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot Bheavy_1 (root_1 r) = Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.bRootPairing r)
  (pairingC_root_1 :
    ∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot C_1 (root_1 r) = Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.cRootPairing r)
  (boundary_1 : Trefoil.RelativeTopology.lensTwoCycles m →ₗ[ZMod 2] Trefoil.RelativeTopology.pieceHandleModule m)
  (inclusion_1 : Trefoil.RelativeTopology.pieceHandleModule m →ₗ[ZMod 2] X)
  (exact_at_pieces_1 : ∀ (z : Trefoil.RelativeTopology.pieceHandleModule m), inclusion_1 z = 0 → ∃ b, boundary_1 b = z)
  (diagonal_coordinates_1 : X ≃ₗ[ZMod 2] Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m → ZMod 2)
  (naturality_1 :
    ∀ (r : Option (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m)) (j : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m),
      diagonal_coordinates_1 (inclusion_1 ((Trefoil.RelativeTopology.canonicalSelectedHandles m) (Finsupp.single r 1)))
          j =
        match r with
        | none => ↑(Bheavy_1 j)
        | some i => ↑(root_1 i j)),
  ({ root := root, A := A, Bheavy := Bheavy, C := C, root_pairing := root_pairing, normA := normA, normB := normB,
        normC := normC, pairingAB := pairingAB, pairingAC := pairingAC, pairingBC := pairingBC,
        pairingA_root := pairingA_root, pairingB_root := pairingB_root, pairingC_root := pairingC_root,
        boundary := boundary, inclusion := inclusion, exact_at_pieces := exact_at_pieces,
        diagonal_coordinates := diagonal_coordinates, naturality := naturality } =
      { root := root_1, A := A_1, Bheavy := Bheavy_1, C := C_1, root_pairing := root_pairing_1, normA := normA_1,
        normB := normB_1, normC := normC_1, pairingAB := pairingAB_1, pairingAC := pairingAC_1,
        pairingBC := pairingBC_1, pairingA_root := pairingA_root_1, pairingB_root := pairingB_root_1,
        pairingC_root := pairingC_root_1, boundary := boundary_1, inclusion := inclusion_1,
        exact_at_pieces := exact_at_pieces_1, diagonal_coordinates := diagonal_coordinates_1,
        naturality := naturality_1 }) =

      A = A_1 ∧
        Bheavy = Bheavy_1 ∧
          C = C_1 ∧ boundary = boundary_1 ∧ inclusion = inclusion_1 ∧ diagonal_coordinates = diagonal_coordinates_1)
