TYPE ∀ {m : ℕ} {X : Type u_2} [inst : AddCommGroup X] [inst_1 : _root_.Module (ZMod 2) X] [inst_2 : SizeOf X]
  (root : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m → Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Vector m)
  (A Bheavy C : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Vector m)
  (root_pairing :
    ∀ (r s : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot (root r) (root s) = Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.rootGram r s)
  (normA : ∑ j, A j ^ 2 = 4) (normB : ∑ j, Bheavy j ^ 2 = 6) (normC : ∑ j, C j ^ 2 = 3)
  (pairingAB : Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot A Bheavy = -1) (pairingAC : Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot A C = 0)
  (pairingBC : Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot Bheavy C = 0)
  (pairingA_root :
    ∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot A (root r) = Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.aRootPairing r)
  (pairingB_root :
    ∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot Bheavy (root r) = Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.bRootPairing r)
  (pairingC_root :
    ∀ (r : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m),
      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot C (root r) = Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.cRootPairing r)
  (boundary : Trefoil.RelativeTopology.lensTwoCycles m →ₗ[ZMod 2] Trefoil.RelativeTopology.pieceHandleModule m)
  (inclusion : Trefoil.RelativeTopology.pieceHandleModule m →ₗ[ZMod 2] X)
  (exact_at_pieces : ∀ (z : Trefoil.RelativeTopology.pieceHandleModule m), inclusion z = 0 → ∃ b, boundary b = z)
  (diagonal_coordinates : X ≃ₗ[ZMod 2] Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m → ZMod 2)
  (naturality :
    ∀ (r : Option (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m)) (j : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m),
      diagonal_coordinates (inclusion ((Trefoil.RelativeTopology.canonicalSelectedHandles m) (Finsupp.single r 1))) j =
        match r with
        | none => ↑(Bheavy j)
        | some i => ↑(root i j)),
  sizeOf
      { root := root, A := A, Bheavy := Bheavy, C := C, root_pairing := root_pairing, normA := normA, normB := normB,
        normC := normC, pairingAB := pairingAB, pairingAC := pairingAC, pairingBC := pairingBC,
        pairingA_root := pairingA_root, pairingB_root := pairingB_root, pairingC_root := pairingC_root,
        boundary := boundary, inclusion := inclusion, exact_at_pieces := exact_at_pieces,
        diagonal_coordinates := diagonal_coordinates, naturality := naturality } =
    1 + sizeOf normA + sizeOf normB + sizeOf normC + sizeOf pairingAB + sizeOf pairingAC + sizeOf pairingBC +
          sizeOf boundary +
        sizeOf inclusion +
      sizeOf diagonal_coordinates

  (diagonal_coordinates : X ≃ₗ[ZMod 2] Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m → ZMod 2)
  (naturality :
    ∀ (r : Option (Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.RootIndex m)) (j : Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m),
      diagonal_coordinates (inclusion (selected_handles (Finsupp.single r 1))) j =
        match r with
        | none => ↑(Bheavy j)
        | some i => ↑(root i j)),
  sizeOf
      { root := root, A := A, Bheavy := Bheavy, C := C, root_pairing := root_pairing, normA := normA, normB := normB,
        normC := normC, pairingAB := pairingAB, pairingAC := pairingAC, pairingBC := pairingBC,
        pairingA_root := pairingA_root, pairingB_root := pairingB_root, pairingC_root := pairingC_root,
        boundary := boundary, inclusion := inclusion, exact_at_pieces := exact_at_pieces,
        selected_handles := selected_handles, selected_handles_injective := selected_handles_injective,
        diagonal_coordinates := diagonal_coordinates, naturality := naturality } =
    1 + sizeOf normA + sizeOf normB + sizeOf normC + sizeOf pairingAB + sizeOf pairingAC + sizeOf pairingBC +
            sizeOf boundary +
          sizeOf inclusion +
        sizeOf selected_handles +
      sizeOf diagonal_coordinates

THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Certificate::Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramGeometry.mk.inj
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ {K : Trefoil.Section2Topology.KnotGeometry} {A : Trefoil.Section2Topology.AugmentedGeometry K}
  {knot : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram → K.Knot}
  {augmented : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram → Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart → A.Link}
  {hasMarkedExterior :
    A.Link → Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation → Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.CuspMarking → ℕ → Prop}
  {hasKnotExterior :
    K.Knot → Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation → Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.CuspMarking → Prop}
  {knot_1 : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram → K.Knot}
  {augmented_1 : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram → Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart → A.Link}
  {hasMarkedExterior_1 :
    A.Link → Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation → Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.CuspMarking → ℕ → Prop}
  {hasKnotExterior_1 :
    K.Knot → Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation → Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.CuspMarking → Prop},
  { knot := knot, augmented := augmented, hasMarkedExterior := hasMarkedExterior, hasKnotExterior := hasKnotExterior } =
      { knot := knot_1, augmented := augmented_1, hasMarkedExterior := hasMarkedExterior_1,
        hasKnotExterior := hasKnotExterior_1 } →
    knot = knot_1 ∧
      augmented = augmented_1 ∧ hasMarkedExterior = hasMarkedExterior_1 ∧ hasKnotExterior = hasKnotExterior_1

    A.Link → Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation → Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.CuspMarking → ℕ → Prop)
  (hasKnotExterior :
    K.Knot → Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation → Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.CuspMarking → Prop)
  (knot_1 : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram → K.Knot)
  (augmented_1 : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram → Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart → A.Link)
  (hasMarkedExterior_1 :
    A.Link → Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation → Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.CuspMarking → ℕ → Prop)
  (hasKnotExterior_1 :
    K.Knot → Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation → Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.CuspMarking → Prop),
  ({ knot := knot, augmented := augmented, hasMarkedExterior := hasMarkedExterior,
        hasKnotExterior := hasKnotExterior } =
      { knot := knot_1, augmented := augmented_1, hasMarkedExterior := hasMarkedExterior_1,
        hasKnotExterior := hasKnotExterior_1 }) =
    (knot = knot_1 ∧
      augmented = augmented_1 ∧ hasMarkedExterior = hasMarkedExterior_1 ∧ hasKnotExterior = hasKnotExterior_1)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2Certificate::Trefoil.Section2Certificate.DiagramGeometry.mk.sizeOf_spec
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ {K : Trefoil.Section2Topology.KnotGeometry} {A : Trefoil.Section2Topology.AugmentedGeometry K}
  (knot : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram → K.Knot)
  (augmented : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram → Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart → A.Link)
  (hasMarkedExterior :
    A.Link → Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation → Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.CuspMarking → ℕ → Prop)
  (hasKnotExterior :
    K.Knot → Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation → Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.CuspMarking → Prop),
  sizeOf
      { knot := knot, augmented := augmented, hasMarkedExterior := hasMarkedExterior,
        hasKnotExterior := hasKnotExterior } =
    1

THEOREM Trefoil.Section2DiagramDomains::Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram.markedDiagram.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (D : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram) (mark : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart),
  D.markedDiagram mark =
    (D.planar && D.numComponents == 2 && D.darts.contains mark &&
      decide
        (List.map Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.vertex
            (Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.visitDarts✝ D.strandNext D.darts.length mark [])).Nodup)

THEOREM Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves::Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.anchor.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (D : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram) (mark : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart),
  Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.anchor D mark [] = if D.darts.contains mark = true then some mark else none
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves::Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.anchor.eq_2
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (D : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram) (mark : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart)
  (fst : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Move) (E : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram)
  (rest : List (Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Move × Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram)),
  Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.anchor D mark ((fst, E) :: rest) =
    if D.darts.contains mark = true then
      (Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Move.transportAnchor mark fst).bind fun mark' =>
        Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.anchor E mark' rest
    else none
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves::Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.check.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (D : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram), Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.check D [] = true
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves::Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.check.eq_2
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (D : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram) (fst : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Move)
  (E : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram)
  (rest : List (Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Move × Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram)),
  Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.check D ((fst, E) :: rest) =
    (E.valid && Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.checkMove D E fst && Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.check E rest)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2DiagramDomains::Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.knot.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (D : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram) (trace : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace),
  Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.knot D trace =
    (D.knotDiagram &&
      List.all trace fun x =>
        match x with
        | (fst, E) => E.knotDiagram)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2DiagramDomains::Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.marked.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (D : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram) (mark : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart),
  Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.marked D mark [] = D.markedDiagram mark
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2DiagramDomains::Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.marked.eq_2
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (D : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram) (mark : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Dart)
  (move : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Move) (E : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram)
  (rest : List (Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Move × Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram)),
  Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.marked D mark ((move, E) :: rest) =
    (D.markedDiagram mark &&
      match Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Move.transportAnchor mark move with
      | none => false
      | some mark' => Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.marked E mark' rest)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section2DiagramDomains::Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.planar.eq_1
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ (D : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Diagram) (trace : Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace),
  Trefoil.Section2DiagramMoves.Trace.planar D trace =
    (D.planar &&
      List.all trace fun x =>
        match x with
        | (fst, E) => E.planar)

  (intermediate_second : A.markedExterior Trefoil.Section2Topology.ExteriorAction.reverse afterSecond beforeThird),
  sizeOf
      { start := start, afterFirst := afterFirst, beforeSecond := beforeSecond, afterSecond := afterSecond,
        beforeThird := beforeThird, finish := finish, residual := residual, first_switch := first_switch,

        final_unlink := final_unlink, residual_reference := residual_reference,
        intermediate_first := intermediate_first, intermediate_second := intermediate_second } =

                        sizeOf finish +

                  sizeOf second_switch +
                sizeOf third_switch +
              sizeOf split_start +
            sizeOf final_unlink +
          sizeOf residual_reference +
        sizeOf intermediate_first +
      sizeOf intermediate_second

  {tetMap : Array Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.TetImage}
  {sourceMarking targetMarking : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.CuspMarking}
  {peripheral : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.PeripheralWitness} {sourceAxis targetAxis : ℕ} {reverse : Bool}
  {source_1 target_1 : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.Triangulation}
  {tetMap_1 : Array Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.TetImage}
  {sourceMarking_1 targetMarking_1 : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.CuspMarking}
  {peripheral_1 : Trefoil.Section2Triangulations.PeripheralWitness} {sourceAxis_1 targetAxis_1 : ℕ} {reverse_1 : Bool},
  { source := source, target := target, tetMap := tetMap, sourceMarking := sourceMarking,
        targetMarking := targetMarking, peripheral := peripheral, sourceAxis := sourceAxis, targetAxis := targetAxis,
        reverse := reverse } =
      { source := source_1, target := target_1, tetMap := tetMap_1, sourceMarking := sourceMarking_1,
        targetMarking := targetMarking_1, peripheral := peripheral_1, sourceAxis := sourceAxis_1,
        targetAxis := targetAxis_1, reverse := reverse_1 } →
    source = source_1 ∧
      target = target_1 ∧
        tetMap = tetMap_1 ∧
          sourceMarking = sourceMarking_1 ∧
            targetMarking = targetMarking_1 ∧

        vertices := vertices, tetrahedronImage := tetrahedronImage, vertexImage := vertexImage } =

        vertexImage := vertexImage_1 } →
    tetrahedra = tetrahedra_1 ∧ vertices = vertices_1

THEOREM Trefoil.Section3ASignature::Trefoil.Section3ASignature.SignatureReferences.mk.inj
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {Knot : Type u_1} {twoBridge : ℕ → ℕ → Knot} {signature : Knot → ℤ} {chainSignature : List ℤ → ℤ}
  {evenPlumbingSignature :
    ∀ (p q : ℕ) (weights : List ℤ),
      0 < q →
        q < p →
          p.Coprime q →
            Odd p →
              (∀ w ∈ weights, Even w) →
                Trefoil.Section3ASignature.MatchesFraction weights p q →
                  signature (twoBridge p q) = chainSignature weights}
  {chainSignature_eq_signedPivotCount :
    ∀ (weights : List ℤ),
      (∀
          pivot ∈
            Trefoil.Arithmetic.rightPivots
              (List.map (fun w => w) do
                let a ← weights
                pure ↑a),
          pivot ≠ 0) →
        chainSignature weights =
          Trefoil.Arithmetic.signedPivotCount
            (Trefoil.Arithmetic.rightPivots
              (List.map (fun w => w) do
                let a ← weights
                pure ↑a))}
  {chainSignature_1 : List ℤ → ℤ}
  {evenPlumbingSignature_1 :
    ∀ (p q : ℕ) (weights : List ℤ),
      0 < q →
        q < p →
          p.Coprime q →
            Odd p →
              (∀ w ∈ weights, Even w) →
                Trefoil.Section3ASignature.MatchesFraction weights p q →
                  signature (twoBridge p q) = chainSignature_1 weights}
  {chainSignature_eq_signedPivotCount_1 :
    ∀ (weights : List ℤ),
      (∀
          pivot ∈
            Trefoil.Arithmetic.rightPivots
              (List.map (fun w => w) do
                let a ← weights
                pure ↑a),
          pivot ≠ 0) →
        chainSignature_1 weights =
          Trefoil.Arithmetic.signedPivotCount
            (Trefoil.Arithmetic.rightPivots
              (List.map (fun w => w) do
                let a ← weights
                pure ↑a))},
  { chainSignature := chainSignature, evenPlumbingSignature := evenPlumbingSignature,
        chainSignature_eq_signedPivotCount := chainSignature_eq_signedPivotCount } =
      { chainSignature := chainSignature_1, evenPlumbingSignature := evenPlumbingSignature_1,
        chainSignature_eq_signedPivotCount := chainSignature_eq_signedPivotCount_1 } →
    chainSignature = chainSignature_1
THEOREM Trefoil.Section3ASignature::Trefoil.Section3ASignature.SignatureReferences.mk.injEq
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {Knot : Type u_1} {twoBridge : ℕ → ℕ → Knot} {signature : Knot → ℤ} (chainSignature : List ℤ → ℤ)
  (evenPlumbingSignature :
    ∀ (p q : ℕ) (weights : List ℤ),
      0 < q →
        q < p →
          p.Coprime q →
            Odd p →
              (∀ w ∈ weights, Even w) →
                Trefoil.Section3ASignature.MatchesFraction weights p q →

  (chainSignature_eq_signedPivotCount :
    ∀ (weights : List ℤ),
      (∀
          pivot ∈
            Trefoil.Arithmetic.rightPivots
              (List.map (fun w => w) do
                let a ← weights
                pure ↑a),

  (chainSignature_1 : List ℤ → ℤ)

        q < p →
          p.Coprime q →
            Odd p →
              (∀ w ∈ weights, Even w) →
                Trefoil.Section3ASignature.MatchesFraction weights p q →
                  signature (twoBridge p q) = chainSignature_1 weights)
  (chainSignature_eq_signedPivotCount_1 :
    ∀ (weights : List ℤ),
      (∀
          pivot ∈
            Trefoil.Arithmetic.rightPivots
              (List.map (fun w => w) do
                let a ← weights
                pure ↑a),
          pivot ≠ 0) →
        chainSignature_1 weights =
          Trefoil.Arithmetic.signedPivotCount
            (Trefoil.Arithmetic.rightPivots
              (List.map (fun w => w) do
                let a ← weights
                pure ↑a))),
  ({ chainSignature := chainSignature, evenPlumbingSignature := evenPlumbingSignature,
        chainSignature_eq_signedPivotCount := chainSignature_eq_signedPivotCount } =
      { chainSignature := chainSignature_1, evenPlumbingSignature := evenPlumbingSignature_1,
        chainSignature_eq_signedPivotCount := chainSignature_eq_signedPivotCount_1 }) =
    (chainSignature = chainSignature_1)

AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {Knot : Type u_1} {twoBridge : ℕ → ℕ → Knot} {signature : Knot → ℤ} [inst : SizeOf Knot]
  (chainSignature : List ℤ → ℤ)
  (evenPlumbingSignature :
    ∀ (p q : ℕ) (weights : List ℤ),
      0 < q →
        q < p →
          p.Coprime q →
            Odd p →
              (∀ w ∈ weights, Even w) →
                Trefoil.Section3ASignature.MatchesFraction weights p q →
                  signature (twoBridge p q) = chainSignature weights)
  (chainSignature_eq_signedPivotCount :
    ∀ (weights : List ℤ),
      (∀
          pivot ∈
            Trefoil.Arithmetic.rightPivots
              (List.map (fun w => w) do
                let a ← weights
                pure ↑a),
          pivot ≠ 0) →
        chainSignature weights =
          Trefoil.Arithmetic.signedPivotCount
            (Trefoil.Arithmetic.rightPivots
              (List.map (fun w => w) do
                let a ← weights
                pure ↑a))),
  sizeOf
      { chainSignature := chainSignature, evenPlumbingSignature := evenPlumbingSignature,
        chainSignature_eq_signedPivotCount := chainSignature_eq_signedPivotCount } =
    1

      { carrier := carrier_1, addGroup := addGroup_1, module := module_1 }) =
    (carrier = carrier_1 ∧ addGroup ≍ addGroup_1 ∧ module ≍ module_1)
THEOREM Trefoil.Section3ATopology::Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Homology.mk.sizeOf_spec
AXIOMS []
TYPE ∀ {R : Type} [inst : Ring R] [inst_1 : SizeOf R] (carrier : Type) [addGroup : AddCommGroup carrier]
  [module : _root_.Module R carrier],
  sizeOf { carrier := carrier, addGroup := addGroup, module := module } =
    1 + sizeOf carrier + sizeOf addGroup + sizeOf module
THEOREM Trefoil.Section3ATopology::Trefoil.Section3ATopology.References.mk.inj
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {T : Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Geometry}
  {sig : Trefoil.Section3ASignature.SignatureReferences T.twoBridge T.signature}
  {mirror_unknotting : ∀ (K : T.Knot), T.unknotting (T.mirror K) = T.unknotting K}
  {conway_fraction :
    ∀ (weights : List ℤ) (p q : ℕ),
      weights ≠ [] →
        (∀ a ∈ weights, 0 < a) →
          Trefoil.Arithmetic.plusPair weights = (↑p, ↑q) →
            Odd p →
              q < p → p.Coprime q → T.conway weights = T.twoBridge p q ∨ T.mirror (T.conway weights) = T.twoBridge p q}
  {bridge_cover : ∀ (p q : ℕ), Odd p → 0 < q → q < p → p.Coprime q → T.doubleCover (T.twoBridge p q) = T.lens p q}
  {minimal_crossing_changes : ∀ (K : T.Knot), ∃ pos neg, pos + neg = T.unknotting K ∧ T.crossingChanges K pos neg}
  {signature_crossing_bound : ∀ (K : T.Knot) (pos neg : ℕ), T.crossingChanges K pos neg → T.signature K ≤ 2 * ↑neg}
  {owens :
    ∀ (K : T.Knot) (pos neg : ℕ),
      T.crossingChanges K pos neg →
        T.signature K = 2 * ↑neg →
          ∃ W,
            T.boundary W.X = T.doubleCover K ∧
              Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Positive T W.X ∧
                (Matrix.det fun i j => ((T.intersection W.X) (W.handle i)) (W.handle j)) =
                    ↑(Nat.card (T.H1BoundaryZ (T.doubleCover K)).carrier) ∧
                  ∀ (i j : Fin (pos + neg)),
                    ((T.intersection W.X) (W.handle (Trefoil.Section3ATopology.meridian i)))
                        (W.handle (Trefoil.Section3ATopology.meridian j)) =
                      if i = j then 2 else 0}
  {chain_plumbing :
    ∀ (weights : List ℤ) (p q : ℕ),
      Trefoil.Arithmetic.minusPair weights = (↑p, ↑q) →
        0 < q →
          q < p →
            p.Coprime q →
              ∃ G,
                T.boundary G.X = T.lens p q ∧
                  ∀ (i j : Fin weights.length),
                    ((T.intersection G.X) (G.handle i)) (G.handle j) = Trefoil.Section3APlumbing.chainGram weights i j}
  {lens_reverse : ∀ (p q : ℕ), 0 < q → q < p → p.Coprime q → T.lens p (p - q) = T.reverse (T.lens p q)}
  {lens_connected : ∀ (p q : ℕ), 0 < q → q < p → p.Coprime q → T.nonemptyPathConnected (T.lens p q)}
  {lens_h1_Z : ∀ (p q : ℕ), 0 < q → q < p → p.Coprime q → Nonempty ((T.H1BoundaryZ (T.lens p q)).carrier ≃ₗ[ℤ] ZMod p)}
  {lens_h2_Z : ∀ (p q : ℕ), 0 < q → q < p → p.Coprime q → Subsingleton (T.H2BoundaryZ (T.lens p q)).carrier}
  {lens_h1_F2 :
    ∀ (p q : ℕ),
      0 < q →
        q < p →
          p.Coprime q →
            Nonempty
              ((T.H1BoundaryF2 (T.lens p q)).carrier ≃ₗ[Trefoil.Section3ATopology.F₂]
                Trefoil.Section3ATopology.F₂ ⧸ LinearMap.range (Trefoil.Section3ATopology.lensD2 p))}
  {lens_h2_F2 :
    ∀ (p q : ℕ),
      0 < q →
        q < p →
          p.Coprime q →
            Nonempty
              ((T.H2BoundaryF2 (T.lens p q)).carrier ≃ₗ[Trefoil.Section3ATopology.F₂]
                ↥(LinearMap.ker (Trefoil.Section3ATopology.lensD2 p)))}
  {glue : (G W : T.Four) → T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W) → Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Gluing T G W}
  {glued_closed : ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)), T.closed (glue G W h).X}
  {glued_simplyConnected :
    ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)),
      T.nonemptyPathConnected (T.boundary W) →
        T.simplyConnected G → T.simplyConnected W → T.simplyConnected (glue G W h).X}
  {glued_intersection :
    ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)) (x y : (T.H2Z G).carrier × (T.H2Z W).carrier),
      ((T.intersection (glue G W h).X) ((glue G W h).inclZ x)) ((glue G W h).inclZ y) =
        ((T.intersection G) x.1) y.1 + ((T.intersection W) x.2) y.2}
  {mv_Z_pieces :
    ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)) (z : (T.H2Z G).carrier × (T.H2Z W).carrier),
      (glue G W h).inclZ z = 0 ↔ ∃ y, (glue G W h).fromBoundaryZ y = z}
  {mv_Z_surjective :
    ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)),
      T.simplyConnected G → T.simplyConnected W → Function.Surjective ⇑(glue G W h).toBoundaryZ}
  {mv_Z :
    ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)) (x : (T.H2Z (glue G W h).X).carrier),
      (glue G W h).toBoundaryZ x = 0 ↔ ∃ z, (glue G W h).inclZ z = x}
  {mv_F2_pieces :
    ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)) (z : (T.H2F2 G).carrier × (T.H2F2 W).carrier),
      (glue G W h).inclF2 z = 0 ↔ ∃ y, (glue G W h).fromBoundaryF2 y = z}
  {mv_F2_closed :
    ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)) (x : (T.H2F2 (glue G W h).X).carrier),
      (glue G W h).toBoundaryF2 x = 0 ↔ ∃ z, (glue G W h).inclF2 z = x}
  {closed_h2_torsionFree :
    ∀ (X : T.Four), T.closed X → T.simplyConnected X → ∀ (k : ℤ) (x : (T.H2Z X).carrier), k ≠ 0 → k • x = 0 → x = 0}
  {uct_surjective : ∀ (X : T.Four), T.simplyConnected X → Function.Surjective ⇑(T.reduce X)}
  {uct_kernel : ∀ (X : T.Four), T.simplyConnected X → ∀ (x : (T.H2Z X).carrier), (T.reduce X) x = 0 ↔ ∃ y, x = y + y}
  {coefficient_naturality :
    ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)) (x : (T.H2Z G).carrier × (T.H2Z W).carrier),
      (T.reduce (glue G W h).X) ((glue G W h).inclZ x) = (glue G W h).inclF2 ((T.reduce G) x.1, (T.reduce W) x.2)}
  {donaldson :
    ∀ (X : T.Four),
      T.closed X →
        T.simplyConnected X →
          Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Positive T X →
            ∃ n e, ∀ (x y : (T.H2Z X).carrier), Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot (e x) (e y) = ((T.intersection X) x) y}
  {sig_1 : Trefoil.Section3ASignature.SignatureReferences T.twoBridge T.signature}
  {mirror_unknotting_1 : ∀ (K : T.Knot), T.unknotting (T.mirror K) = T.unknotting K}
  {conway_fraction_1 :
    ∀ (weights : List ℤ) (p q : ℕ),
      weights ≠ [] →
        (∀ a ∈ weights, 0 < a) →

            Odd p →
              q < p → p.Coprime q → T.conway weights = T.twoBridge p q ∨ T.mirror (T.conway weights) = T.twoBridge p q}
  {bridge_cover_1 : ∀ (p q : ℕ), Odd p → 0 < q → q < p → p.Coprime q → T.doubleCover (T.twoBridge p q) = T.lens p q}
  {minimal_crossing_changes_1 : ∀ (K : T.Knot), ∃ pos neg, pos + neg = T.unknotting K ∧ T.crossingChanges K pos neg}
  {signature_crossing_bound_1 : ∀ (K : T.Knot) (pos neg : ℕ), T.crossingChanges K pos neg → T.signature K ≤ 2 * ↑neg}
  {owens_1 :
    ∀ (K : T.Knot) (pos neg : ℕ),
      T.crossingChanges K pos neg →

          ∃ W,
            T.boundary W.X = T.doubleCover K ∧

                    ((T.intersection W.X) (W.handle (Trefoil.Section3ATopology.meridian i)))
                        (W.handle (Trefoil.Section3ATopology.meridian j)) =

  {lens_reverse_1 : ∀ (p q : ℕ), 0 < q → q < p → p.Coprime q → T.lens p (p - q) = T.reverse (T.lens p q)}
  {lens_connected_1 : ∀ (p q : ℕ), 0 < q → q < p → p.Coprime q → T.nonemptyPathConnected (T.lens p q)}

  (glued_closed_1 : ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)), T.closed (glue_1 G W h).X)
  (glued_simplyConnected_1 :
    ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)),
      T.nonemptyPathConnected (T.boundary W) →
        T.simplyConnected G → T.simplyConnected W → T.simplyConnected (glue_1 G W h).X)
  (glued_intersection_1 :
    ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)) (x y : (T.H2Z G).carrier × (T.H2Z W).carrier), ⋯ = ⋯)
  (mv_Z_pieces_1 :
    ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)) (z : (T.H2Z G).carrier × (T.H2Z W).carrier), ⋯)
  (mv_Z_surjective_1 : ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)), ⋯) (mv_Z_1 : ∀ (G W : T.Four), ⋯)
  (mv_F2_pieces_1 : ⋯), ⋯
THEOREM Trefoil.Section3ATopology::Trefoil.Section3ATopology.References.mk.sizeOf_spec
AXIOMS [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
TYPE ∀ {T : Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Geometry}
  (sig : Trefoil.Section3ASignature.SignatureReferences T.twoBridge T.signature)
  (mirror_unknotting : ∀ (K : T.Knot), T.unknotting (T.mirror K) = T.unknotting K)
  (conway_fraction :
    ∀ (weights : List ℤ) (p q : ℕ),
      weights ≠ [] →
        (∀ a ∈ weights, 0 < a) →
          Trefoil.Arithmetic.plusPair weights = (↑p, ↑q) →
            Odd p →
              q < p → p.Coprime q → T.conway weights = T.twoBridge p q ∨ T.mirror (T.conway weights) = T.twoBridge p q)
  (bridge_cover : ∀ (p q : ℕ), Odd p → 0 < q → q < p → p.Coprime q → T.doubleCover (T.twoBridge p q) = T.lens p q)
  (minimal_crossing_changes : ∀ (K : T.Knot), ∃ pos neg, pos + neg = T.unknotting K ∧ T.crossingChanges K pos neg)
  (signature_crossing_bound : ∀ (K : T.Knot) (pos neg : ℕ), T.crossingChanges K pos neg → T.signature K ≤ 2 * ↑neg)
  (owens :
    ∀ (K : T.Knot) (pos neg : ℕ),
      T.crossingChanges K pos neg →
        T.signature K = 2 * ↑neg →
          ∃ W,
            T.boundary W.X = T.doubleCover K ∧
              Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Positive T W.X ∧
                (Matrix.det fun i j => ((T.intersection W.X) (W.handle i)) (W.handle j)) =
                    ↑(Nat.card (T.H1BoundaryZ (T.doubleCover K)).carrier) ∧
                  ∀ (i j : Fin (pos + neg)),
                    ((T.intersection W.X) (W.handle (Trefoil.Section3ATopology.meridian i)))
                        (W.handle (Trefoil.Section3ATopology.meridian j)) =
                      if i = j then 2 else 0)
  (chain_plumbing :
    ∀ (weights : List ℤ) (p q : ℕ),
      Trefoil.Arithmetic.minusPair weights = (↑p, ↑q) →
        0 < q →
          q < p →
            p.Coprime q →
              ∃ G,
                T.boundary G.X = T.lens p q ∧
                  ∀ (i j : Fin weights.length),
                    ((T.intersection G.X) (G.handle i)) (G.handle j) = Trefoil.Section3APlumbing.chainGram weights i j)
  (lens_reverse : ∀ (p q : ℕ), 0 < q → q < p → p.Coprime q → T.lens p (p - q) = T.reverse (T.lens p q))
  (lens_connected : ∀ (p q : ℕ), 0 < q → q < p → p.Coprime q → T.nonemptyPathConnected (T.lens p q))
  (lens_h1_Z : ∀ (p q : ℕ), 0 < q → q < p → p.Coprime q → Nonempty ((T.H1BoundaryZ (T.lens p q)).carrier ≃ₗ[ℤ] ZMod p))
  (lens_h2_Z : ∀ (p q : ℕ), 0 < q → q < p → p.Coprime q → Subsingleton (T.H2BoundaryZ (T.lens p q)).carrier)
  (lens_h1_F2 :
    ∀ (p q : ℕ),
      0 < q →
        q < p →
          p.Coprime q →
            Nonempty
              ((T.H1BoundaryF2 (T.lens p q)).carrier ≃ₗ[Trefoil.Section3ATopology.F₂]
                Trefoil.Section3ATopology.F₂ ⧸ LinearMap.range (Trefoil.Section3ATopology.lensD2 p)))
  (lens_h2_F2 :
    ∀ (p q : ℕ),
      0 < q →
        q < p →
          p.Coprime q →
            Nonempty
              ((T.H2BoundaryF2 (T.lens p q)).carrier ≃ₗ[Trefoil.Section3ATopology.F₂]
                ↥(LinearMap.ker (Trefoil.Section3ATopology.lensD2 p))))
  (glue : (G W : T.Four) → T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W) → Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Gluing T G W)
  (glued_closed : ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)), T.closed (glue G W h).X)
  (glued_simplyConnected :
    ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)),
      T.nonemptyPathConnected (T.boundary W) →
        T.simplyConnected G → T.simplyConnected W → T.simplyConnected (glue G W h).X)
  (glued_intersection :
    ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)) (x y : (T.H2Z G).carrier × (T.H2Z W).carrier),
      ((T.intersection (glue G W h).X) ((glue G W h).inclZ x)) ((glue G W h).inclZ y) =
        ((T.intersection G) x.1) y.1 + ((T.intersection W) x.2) y.2)
  (mv_Z_pieces :
    ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)) (z : (T.H2Z G).carrier × (T.H2Z W).carrier),
      (glue G W h).inclZ z = 0 ↔ ∃ y, (glue G W h).fromBoundaryZ y = z)
  (mv_Z_surjective :
    ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)),
      T.simplyConnected G → T.simplyConnected W → Function.Surjective ⇑(glue G W h).toBoundaryZ)
  (mv_Z :
    ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)) (x : (T.H2Z (glue G W h).X).carrier),
      (glue G W h).toBoundaryZ x = 0 ↔ ∃ z, (glue G W h).inclZ z = x)
  (mv_F2_pieces :
    ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)) (z : (T.H2F2 G).carrier × (T.H2F2 W).carrier),
      (glue G W h).inclF2 z = 0 ↔ ∃ y, (glue G W h).fromBoundaryF2 y = z)
  (mv_F2_closed :
    ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)) (x : (T.H2F2 (glue G W h).X).carrier),
      (glue G W h).toBoundaryF2 x = 0 ↔ ∃ z, (glue G W h).inclF2 z = x)
  (closed_h2_torsionFree :
    ∀ (X : T.Four), T.closed X → T.simplyConnected X → ∀ (k : ℤ) (x : (T.H2Z X).carrier), k ≠ 0 → k • x = 0 → x = 0)
  (uct_surjective : ∀ (X : T.Four), T.simplyConnected X → Function.Surjective ⇑(T.reduce X))
  (uct_kernel : ∀ (X : T.Four), T.simplyConnected X → ∀ (x : (T.H2Z X).carrier), (T.reduce X) x = 0 ↔ ∃ y, x = y + y)
  (coefficient_naturality :
    ∀ (G W : T.Four) (h : T.boundary G = T.reverse (T.boundary W)) (x : (T.H2Z G).carrier × (T.H2Z W).carrier),
      (T.reduce (glue G W h).X) ((glue G W h).inclZ x) = (glue G W h).inclF2 ((T.reduce G) x.1, (T.reduce W) x.2))
  (donaldson :
    ∀ (X : T.Four),
      T.closed X →
        T.simplyConnected X →
          Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Positive T X →
            ∃ n e, ∀ (x y : (T.H2Z X).carrier), Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot (e x) (e y) = ((T.intersection X) x) y),
  sizeOf
      { sig := sig, mirror_unknotting := mirror_unknotting, conway_fraction := conway_fraction,
        bridge_cover := bridge_cover, minimal_crossing_changes := minimal_crossing_changes,
        signature_crossing_bound := signature_crossing_bound, owens := owens, chain_plumbing := chain_plumbing,
        lens_reverse := lens_reverse, lens_connected := lens_connected, lens_h1_Z := lens_h1_Z, lens_h2_Z := lens_h2_Z,
        lens_h1_F2 := lens_h1_F2, lens_h2_F2 := lens_h2_F2, glue := glue, glued_closed := glued_closed,
        glued_simplyConnected := glued_simplyConnected, glued_intersection := glued_intersection,
        mv_Z_pieces := mv_Z_pieces, mv_Z_surjective := mv_Z_surjective, mv_Z := mv_Z, mv_F2_pieces := mv_F2_pieces,
        mv_F2_closed := mv_F2_closed, closed_h2_torsionFree := closed_h2_torsionFree, uct_surjective := uct_surjective,
        uct_kernel := uct_kernel, coefficient_naturality := coefficient_naturality, donaldson := donaldson } =
    1 + sizeOf sig

TYPE ∀ {delta : ℕ} {h a s : ℤ} {d : ZMod delta → ℚ}
  {classIso : Trefoil.Section3BMinimum.SurgeryCokernel h a s ≃+ ZMod delta} {minimum : ZMod delta → ℚ}
  {attained :
    ∀ (x : Trefoil.Finite.ZVector) (z : Trefoil.Finite.QVector),
      ((Trefoil.Finite.surgeryMatrix h a s).mulVec z = fun i => ↑(x i)) →
        ∃ y,
          (Trefoil.Finite.quadraticBound (Trefoil.Section3BMinimum.surgeryTranslate h a s x y) fun i => z i + ↑(y i)) =
            minimum (classIso ((Trefoil.Section3BMinimum.cokernelClass h a s) x))}
  {minimizing :
    ∀ (x : Trefoil.Finite.ZVector) (z : Trefoil.Finite.QVector),
      ((Trefoil.Finite.surgeryMatrix h a s).mulVec z = fun i => ↑(x i)) →
        minimum (classIso ((Trefoil.Section3BMinimum.cokernelClass h a s) x)) ≤ Trefoil.Finite.quadraticBound x z}
  {floerComparison : ∀ (j : ZMod delta), ∃ n, minimum j - d j = 2 * ↑n}
  {classIso_1 : Trefoil.Section3BMinimum.SurgeryCokernel h a s ≃+ ZMod delta} {minimum_1 : ZMod delta → ℚ}

        minimum_1 (classIso_1 ((Trefoil.Section3BMinimum.cokernelClass h a s) x)) ≤ Trefoil.Finite.quadraticBound x z}
  {floerComparison_1 : ∀ (j : ZMod delta), ∃ n, minimum_1 j - d j = 2 * ↑n},

        floerComparison := floerComparison } =
      { classIso := classIso_1, minimum := minimum_1, attained := attained_1, minimizing := minimizing_1,
        floerComparison := floerComparison_1 } →
    classIso = classIso_1 ∧ minimum = minimum_1

TYPE ∀ {T : Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Geometry} {alternating : T.Knot → Prop}
  {twoBridge_alternating : ∀ (p q : ℕ), Odd p → 0 < q → q < p → p.Coprime q → alternating (T.twoBridge p q)}
  {correction : T.Three → (p : ℕ) → ℕ → ZMod p → ℚ} {rawLensLabel : (p : ℕ) → ℕ → Fin p → ZMod p}
  {rawLabel_difference :
    ∀ (p t : ℕ),
      Odd p → 0 < t → t < p → p.Coprime t → ∀ (i k : Fin p), rawLensLabel p t i - rawLensLabel p t k = ↑↑i - ↑↑k}
  {rawLabel_fixed_conjugation :
    ∀ (p t : ℕ),
      Odd p →
        0 < t →
          t < p → p.Coprime t → ∀ (i : Fin p), 2 * ↑i % p = (p + t - 1) % p → rawLensLabel p t i = -rawLensLabel p t i}
  {osz_negative_lens :
    ∀ (p t : ℕ),
      Odd p →
        0 < t →
          t < p →
            p.Coprime t →
              ∀ (i : Fin p),
                correction (T.reverse (T.lens p t)) p t (rawLensLabel p t i) = Trefoil.Finite.correctionTerm p t ↑i}
  {goeritzMinimum : T.Knot → (p : ℕ) → ℕ → ZMod p → ℚ}
  {goeritz_sharp :
    ∀ (K : T.Knot) (p q : ℕ),
      alternating K →
        T.doubleCover K = T.lens p q →
          Odd p →
            0 < q →
              q < p →
                p.Coprime q → ∀ (j : ZMod p), goeritzMinimum K p (p - q) j = correction (T.doubleCover K) p (p - q) j}
  {owens_alternating :
    ∀ (K : T.Knot) (p q : ℕ),
      alternating K →
        T.signature K = 4 →
          T.crossingChanges K 0 2 →
            T.doubleCover K = T.lens p q →
              Odd p →
                0 < q →
                  q < p →
                    p.Coprime q →
                      ∃ h a s,
                        0 ≤ a ∧
                          a < h ∧
                            h ≤ s ∧
                              Even h ∧
                                Even s ∧
                                  (Trefoil.Section3BMinimum.surgeryMatrixZ h a s).det = ↑p ∧
                                    Nonempty
                                      (Trefoil.Section3BMinimum.OwensMinimum p h a s (goeritzMinimum K p (p - q)))}
  {alternating_1 : T.Knot → Prop}
  {twoBridge_alternating_1 : ∀ (p q : ℕ), Odd p → 0 < q → q < p → p.Coprime q → alternating_1 (T.twoBridge p q)}
  {correction_1 : T.Three → (p : ℕ) → ℕ → ZMod p → ℚ} {rawLensLabel_1 : (p : ℕ) → ℕ → Fin p → ZMod p}
  {rawLabel_difference_1 :

      Odd p → 0 < t → t < p → p.Coprime t → ∀ (i k : Fin p), rawLensLabel_1 p t i - rawLensLabel_1 p t k = ↑↑i - ↑↑k}
  {rawLabel_fixed_conjugation_1 :

            p.Coprime t → ∀ (i : Fin p), 2 * ↑i % p = (p + t - 1) % p → rawLensLabel_1 p t i = -rawLensLabel_1 p t i}
  {osz_negative_lens_1 :
    ∀ (p t : ℕ),
      Odd p →
        0 < t →
          t < p →
            p.Coprime t →
              ∀ (i : Fin p),
                correction_1 (T.reverse (T.lens p t)) p t (rawLensLabel_1 p t i) = Trefoil.Finite.correctionTerm p t ↑i}
  {goeritzMinimum_1 : T.Knot → (p : ℕ) → ℕ → ZMod p → ℚ}
  {goeritz_sharp_1 :
    ∀ (K : T.Knot) (p q : ℕ),
      alternating_1 K →
        T.doubleCover K = T.lens p q →
          Odd p →
            0 < q →
              q < p →
                p.Coprime q →
                  ∀ (j : ZMod p), goeritzMinimum_1 K p (p - q) j = correction_1 (T.doubleCover K) p (p - q) j}
  {owens_alternating_1 :
    ∀ (K : T.Knot) (p q : ℕ),
      alternating_1 K →
        T.signature K = 4 →
          T.crossingChanges K 0 2 →
            T.doubleCover K = T.lens p q →
              Odd p →
                0 < q →
                  q < p →
                    p.Coprime q →
                      ∃ h a s,
                        0 ≤ a ∧
                          a < h ∧
                            h ≤ s ∧
                              Even h ∧
                                Even s ∧
                                  (Trefoil.Section3BMinimum.surgeryMatrixZ h a s).det = ↑p ∧
                                    Nonempty
                                      (Trefoil.Section3BMinimum.OwensMinimum p h a s (goeritzMinimum_1 K p (p - q)))},
  { alternating := alternating, twoBridge_alternating := twoBridge_alternating, correction := correction,
        rawLensLabel := rawLensLabel, rawLabel_difference := rawLabel_difference,
        rawLabel_fixed_conjugation := rawLabel_fixed_conjugation, osz_negative_lens := osz_negative_lens,
        goeritzMinimum := goeritzMinimum, goeritz_sharp := goeritz_sharp, owens_alternating := owens_alternating } =
      { alternating := alternating_1, twoBridge_alternating := twoBridge_alternating_1, correction := correction_1,

      correction = correction_1 ∧ rawLensLabel = rawLensLabel_1 ∧ goeritzMinimum = goeritzMinimum_1

TYPE ∀ {T : Trefoil.Section3ATopology.Geometry} (alternating : T.Knot → Prop)

            p.Coprime t →
              ∀ (i : Fin p),
                correction (T.reverse (T.lens p t)) p t (rawLensLabel p t i) = Trefoil.Finite.correctionTerm p t ↑i)
  (goeritzMinimum : T.Knot → (p : ℕ) → ℕ → ZMod p → ℚ)
  (goeritz_sharp :
    ∀ (K : T.Knot) (p q : ℕ),
      alternating K →
        T.doubleCover K = T.lens p q →
          Odd p →
            0 < q →
              q < p →
                p.Coprime q → ∀ (j : ZMod p), goeritzMinimum K p (p - q) j = correction (T.doubleCover K) p (p - q) j)
  (owens_alternating :
    ∀ (K : T.Knot) (p q : ℕ),
      alternating K →
        T.signature K = 4 →
          T.crossingChanges K 0 2 →
            T.doubleCover K = T.lens p q →
              Odd p →
                0 < q →
                  q < p →
                    p.Coprime q →
                      ∃ h a s,
                        0 ≤ a ∧
                          a < h ∧
                            h ≤ s ∧
                              Even h ∧
                                Even s ∧
                                  (Trefoil.Section3BMinimum.surgeryMatrixZ h a s).det = ↑p ∧
                                    Nonempty
                                      (Trefoil.Section3BMinimum.OwensMinimum p h a s (goeritzMinimum K p (p - q))))
  (alternating_1 : T.Knot → Prop)
  (twoBridge_alternating_1 : ∀ (p q : ℕ), Odd p → 0 < q → q < p → p.Coprime q → alternating_1 (T.twoBridge p q))
  (correction_1 : T.Three → (p : ℕ) → ℕ → ZMod p → ℚ) (rawLensLabel_1 : (p : ℕ) → ℕ → Fin p → ZMod p)
  (rawLabel_difference_1 :
    ∀ (p t : ℕ),
      Odd p → 0 < t → t < p → p.Coprime t → ∀ (i k : Fin p), rawLensLabel_1 p t i - rawLensLabel_1 p t k = ↑↑i - ↑↑k)
  (rawLabel_fixed_conjugation_1 :
    ∀ (p t : ℕ),
      Odd p →
        0 < t →
          t < p →
            p.Coprime t → ∀ (i : Fin p), 2 * ↑i % p = (p + t - 1) % p → rawLensLabel_1 p t i = -rawLensLabel_1 p t i)
  (osz_negative_lens_1 :
    ∀ (p t : ℕ),
      Odd p →
        0 < t →
          t < p →
            p.Coprime t →
              ∀ (i : Fin p),
                correction_1 (T.reverse (T.lens p t)) p t (rawLensLabel_1 p t i) = Trefoil.Finite.correctionTerm p t ↑i)
  (goeritzMinimum_1 : T.Knot → (p : ℕ) → ℕ → ZMod p → ℚ)
  (goeritz_sharp_1 :
    ∀ (K : T.Knot) (p q : ℕ),
      alternating_1 K →
        T.doubleCover K = T.lens p q →
          Odd p →
            0 < q →
              q < p →
                p.Coprime q →
                  ∀ (j : ZMod p), goeritzMinimum_1 K p (p - q) j = correction_1 (T.doubleCover K) p (p - q) j)
  (owens_alternating_1 :
    ∀ (K : T.Knot) (p q : ℕ),
      alternating_1 K →
        T.signature K = 4 →
          T.crossingChanges K 0 2 →
            T.doubleCover K = T.lens p q →
              Odd p →
                0 < q →
                  q < p →
                    p.Coprime q →
                      ∃ h a s,
                        0 ≤ a ∧
                          a < h ∧
                            h ≤ s ∧
                              Even h ∧
                                Even s ∧
                                  (Trefoil.Section3BMinimum.surgeryMatrixZ h a s).det = ↑p ∧
                                    Nonempty
                                      (Trefoil.Section3BMinimum.OwensMinimum p h a s (goeritzMinimum_1 K p (p - q)))),
  ({ alternating := alternating, twoBridge_alternating := twoBridge_alternating, correction := correction,
        rawLensLabel := rawLensLabel, rawLabel_difference := rawLabel_difference,
        rawLabel_fixed_conjugation := rawLabel_fixed_conjugation, osz_negative_lens := osz_negative_lens,
        goeritzMinimum := goeritzMinimum, goeritz_sharp := goeritz_sharp, owens_alternating := owens_alternating } =
      { alternating := alternating_1, twoBridge_alternating := twoBridge_alternating_1, correction := correction_1,
        rawLensLabel := rawLensLabel_1, rawLabel_difference := rawLabel_difference_1,
        rawLabel_fixed_conjugation := rawLabel_fixed_conjugation_1, osz_negative_lens := osz_negative_lens_1,
        goeritzMinimum := goeritzMinimum_1, goeritz_sharp := goeritz_sharp_1,
        owens_alternating := owens_alternating_1 }) =
    (alternating = alternating_1 ∧
      correction = correction_1 ∧ rawLensLabel = rawLensLabel_1 ∧ goeritzMinimum = goeritzMinimum_1)

  {p : ℕ → Trefoil.LatticeEmbedding.Ambient m} {eps : ℕ → ℤ}
  {support : ∀ (i : Fin k), Trefoil.LatticeSupports.support (E.root (r i)) = {p ↑i, p (↑i + 1)}}
  {injective : ∀ (i j : ℕ), i ≤ k → j ≤ k → p i = p j → i = j} {unit : ∀ j ≤ k, eps j ^ 2 = 1}
  {pairing :

      Trefoil.LatticeSupports.dot x (E.root (r i)) = eps ↑i * x (p ↑i) - eps (↑i + 1) * x (p (↑i + 1))}
